Abstract: Exploring unknown environments with multiple UAVs requires coordination under intermittent communication, making decentralized operation a baseline assumption. We propose, within a decentralized framework, a novel exploration graph extension strategy based on frontier connectivity to extend exploration plans and maintain area partitioning among agents stable and robust to disconnections and changes in spatial layout. The proposed extension method is applied to two state-of-the-art area partitioning methods and evaluated in simulation. Experiments show improved performance over existing graph extension approaches with higher exploration efficiency under low communication rate.

</details>


### [138] [Peg-in-Bench: A Modular Benchmark for High-Precision Robotic Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00906)
*Yosel Delgado,José G. Buenaventura-Carreón,Floris Erich,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Tomohiro Motoda,Koshi Makihara,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可重构的销孔插入（peg-in-hole）基准测试，用于评估高精度插入任务中的泛化能力。该基准包含完全3D可打印的模块化组件，可组合生成多种插入和装配任务，并提供场景生成工具以确保标准化和可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度插入是机器人操作中的基础挑战，但现有评估基准依赖固定任务配置，无法充分评估机器人在不同插入场景下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 设计并实现了一个可重构的peg-in-hole基准，包括多种销钉几何形状、不同公差等级和可配置底座结构等3D可打印模块化组件，通过改变物体布局、方向和任务结构来系统性地生成多样化且具挑战性的组装任务；同时提供场景生成工具，可输出标准化任务配置和机器可读的任务描述。

Result: 该基准能够生成大量多样化的插入与装配任务，在受控物理条件下系统评估策略对未见场景的适应能力，并通过公开的场景生成工具和STL文件保证可复现性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了高精度机器人插入任务中泛化评估基准的空白，为社区提供了标准化、可扩展且可复现的评估平台。

Abstract: High-precision insertion remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation due to the strict alignment requirements and contact-rich interactions involved. Although peg-in-hole tasks are widely used for evaluation, existing bench- marks often rely on fixed task configurations, limiting their ability to assess robustness and generalization across different insertion scenarios. This paper introduces a reconfigurable peg-in-hole benchmark designed to evaluate task generalization in high-precision insertion. The benchmark consists of a set of fully 3D-printable modular components, including multiple peg geometries, tolerance levels, and configurable base structures that can be combined to generate a large variety of insertion and assembly tasks. By varying object layouts, orientations, and task structures while maintaining controlled physical conditions, the benchmark enables systematic evaluation of adaptation to unseen scenarios. To support reproducibility, we additionally provide a scenario generation tool capable of producing standardized task configurations and machine-readable task descriptions. The scenario generation tool and the STL files of the benchmark pieces are available through the project repository: https://github.com/aistairc/peg-in-bench.

</details>


### [139] [Knowing When to Stop: Adaptive Action Chunking via Internal Cross-Attention Dynamics in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00908)
*Runze Xu,Xiaolong Shan,Shuang Dai,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动作专家内部交叉注意力动态的自适应动作分块（adaptive action chunking）方法。通过检测动作到观测交叉注意力的熵值在预测范围延伸时趋于高熵平台期的现象，利用训练无关的截断机制在推理时动态选择执行水平线，从而在保持闭环控制效率的同时提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLA框架中固定动作分块（fixed action chunking）存在效率与准确性的权衡：短分块需要频繁推理且可能导致振荡行为，长分块则可能与新观察状态失配。需要一种自适应机制来动态调整执行水平线。

Method: 观察到动作到观察（action-to-observation）交叉注意力随预测范围延伸而趋于分散、熵上升至平台期的模式，该模式与动作预测误差升高相关。基于此引入一个无需训练的截断机制：在推理过程中检测持续的高熵平台期，动态决定执行水平线。该方法利用策略中已计算的注意力权重，额外开销可忽略。

Result: 在π0.5和X-VLA框架上，针对RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO及三个真实世界操作任务进行评测，相比固定水平线和自适应分块基线，平均任务成功率得到提升，同时保持了高效的闭环控制。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力动态可以作为VLA中自适应动作执行的一种实用内部信号，为在训练无关条件下动态调整动作分块水平线提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Action chunking is a standard execution strategy in modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks, but fixed execution horizons impose a trade-off between efficiency and accuracy. Short chunks require frequent inference and may cause oscillatory behavior, whereas long chunks can become misaligned with newly observed states. We address this limitation with an adaptive action chunking approach based on internal cross-attention dynamics in the action expert. We observe that, as the prediction horizon extends, action-to-observation cross-attention becomes increasingly dispersed and its entropy rises toward a plateau. This pattern is associated with higher action prediction error and provides an online signal that the current observation offers limited grounding for further open-loop execution. Based on this observation, we introduce a training-free truncation mechanism that detects sustained high-entropy plateaus and dynamically selects the execution horizon during inference. The method uses attention weights already computed by the policy and introduces negligible additional overhead. Evaluations on $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA across RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO, and three real-world manipulation tasks show improved average task success over fixed-horizon and adaptive chunking baselines, while preserving efficient closed-loop control. These results show that cross-attention dynamics can provide a practical internal signal for adaptive action execution in VLAs.

</details>


### [140] [VerNav: Verifier-First Low-Latency Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00920)
*Zhixin Wang,Chengzheyi Yao,Leyuan Liu,Xiaosong Zhang,Yongzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VerNav是一个面向低延迟LLM-based VLN的verifier-first框架，通过批量动作验证替代逐步自回归生成来降低决策延迟，仅对不确定决策调用自适应生成器，并利用验证器偏好优化（VPO）和步骤级强化微调两阶段对齐方案提升导航性能。在R2R基准测试中，VerNav的纯验证器决策路径在保持竞争力的同时，将每步LLM延迟降低了10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-based视觉语言导航（VLN）虽然通过显式推理提升了指令理解和语义接地能力，但每一步都进行自回归生成会导致多步导航过程中决策阶段延迟累积严重，难以满足实时导航的需求。

Method: (1) 提出verifier-first框架，用批量动作验证替代每步自回归生成，降低决策延迟；(2) 设计基于熵的自适应生成器，仅在不确定性高的决策时调用以产生紧凑状态证据；(3) 提出两阶段对齐方案：VPO（验证器偏好优化）用于静态验证器训练中的局部动作偏好对齐，步骤级强化微调在动态任务执行的导航轨迹中提供密集进度奖励。

Result: 在Room-to-Room (R2R)基准上，VerNav的纯验证器决策路径在代表性LLM-based VLN智能体中取得了有竞争力的导航性能，同时将平均每步决策阶段的LLM延迟相比自回归方法降低了10倍以上。

Conclusion: VerNav验证了verifier-first范式在VLN中的有效性：通过将推理负担从每步生成转变为批量验证，能够在保持导航性能的同时大幅降低延迟，为实时LLM-based导航系统提供了一条可行的路径。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate through unseen 3D environments according to natural-language instructions. Explicit reasoning can improve instruction understanding and semantic grounding, but autoregressive generation at every step accumulates large decision-stage latency over multi-step navigation. We propose VerNav, a verifier-first framework for low-latency LLM-based VLN. The verifier reduces decision-stage latency by replacing per-step autoregressive generation with batched action verification, while an entropy-based adaptive generator is invoked only for uncertain decisions to produce compact state evidence. To further improve navigation performance with the verifier, we introduce a two-stage alignment scheme: (i) VPO improves local action-preference alignment in static verifier training, and (ii) step-level reinforcement fine-tuning provides dense progress rewards over multi-step navigation rollouts during dynamic task execution. Experiments on the Room-to-Room (R2R) benchmark show that the verifier-only decision path of VerNav achieves competitive navigation performance among representative LLM-based VLN agents while reducing average decision-stage LLM latency per step by more than $10\times$ compared with autoregressive methods.

</details>


### [141] [ProxPI: Proximal Prior Injection for Sampling-Based MPC under Learned-Prior Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00941)
*Euncheol Im,Myotaeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI)方法，通过在标称中心化MPPI采样中引入软近程代价来注入策略先验，替代现有将采样分布中心化在策略输出上的做法。该方法在策略分布内任务上保持与现有方案相当的性能，同时在策略失配时能够逃离不良先验并恢复至vanilla MPPI水平，理论分析与仿真/实物实验均验证了其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略引导的模型预测路径积分控制（policy-guided MPPI）虽然能结合学习先验与在线优化能力，但当学习策略分布外时，将采样分布中心化在策略输出上会限制探索空间，阻止优化器向任务最优点恢复，导致性能严重退化。

Method: 提出ProxPI：保留以标称（nominal）为中心的MPPI采样方式，不改变采样分布中心，而是将学习策略通过一个软近程代价（soft proximity cost）集成到代价函数中，从而引导优化器偏向策略建议但保留偏离能力。理论分析对比了prior重新中心化与标称中心化两种采样方案的差异。

Result: ProxPI在策略分布内任务上达到与现有先验注入方案相当的性能；在分布外任务中，能够克服失配的先验并恢复至vanilla MPPI级别的性能。理论证明表明重新中心化到先验会在每次更新时丢弃优化器的修正量，而标称中心化采样能保留修正并收敛到任务代价与先验共同确定的解集，且增加rollout预算无法消除该缺陷。仿真与实物机器人实验验证了其在分布内与分布外任务中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ProxPI通过在代价层而非采样分布层注入策略先验，兼顾了分布内性能与分布外鲁棒性，为策略与MPC的融合提供了一种更稳健的范式。

Abstract: Combining learned policies with model predictive control can leverage learned task priors while retaining online adaptation to new objectives and constraints, but performance degrades when the policy is out of distribution. In policy-guided model predictive path integral (MPPI) control, a policy-centered warm-start approach centers the sampling distribution on the policy output. When the prior is mismatched, centering the sampling distribution on the policy output restricts exploration around an unsuitable solution and prevents recovery toward the task optimum. We propose Proximal Prior Injection (ProxPI), which retains nominal-centered MPPI sampling and incorporates the policy through a soft proximity cost. This matches the in-distribution performance of existing prior-injection schemes while enabling the optimizer to escape an inaccurate policy and recover vanilla MPPI-level performance. We theoretically show that re-centering on the prior discards the optimizer's correction at every update, whereas nominal-centered sampling retains it and converges to a solution set by both the task cost and the prior, and that this failure is not removed by a larger rollout budget. Simulations and real-robot experiments demonstrate robust performance under both in-distribution and out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [142] [HitMem: Hierarchical Temporal 3D Memory with Multi-Modal Context-Aware Retrieval for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00950)
*Ruijie Tang,Chenye Zou,Guoquan Wu,Jun Wei,Wei Chen,Jiaxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HitMem是一个层次化时序3D记忆框架，通过轻量拓扑图与时间衰减机制维持动态环境中的场景表征，并在检测到物体位移时启用两阶段检索机制（结合外部代理轨迹的空间约束与类别亲和性的语义常识）来高效定位物体，在Dyna-THOR基准上显著提升了动态环境下的物体重定位精度、降低了探索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数现有3D记忆框架基于静态世界假设，难以应对由人类活动或未观测事件导致的物体位置变化，智能体会遭遇记忆-观测冲突，需要昂贵的几何重计算或低效的全局重探索。

Method: 提出HitMem层次化时序3D记忆框架：(1)通过连续感知将语义与空间信息统一为捕获支撑关系的轻量拓扑图；(2)时间衰减机制动态调节记忆活跃度以缓解陈旧表征影响；(3)多模态上下文感知检索机制，默认使用语义、空间与时间特征过滤候选，检测到物体位移时激活两阶段检索，结合外部代理轨迹的空间约束与类别亲和性的语义常识定位高概率候选区域。

Result: 在作者构建的Dyna-THOR基准上，HitMem显著提高了物体重定位准确率，降低了探索成本，并提升了动态环境中的任务执行性能。

Conclusion: HitMem通过层次化拓扑记忆与时序衰减机制，辅以多模态上下文感知的双阶段检索策略，为具身智能体在动态场景中维护自适应3D记忆提供了有效方案，兼顾了表征轻量性与检索效率。

Abstract: Executing long-term tasks in dynamic environments requires embodied agents to maintain robust and adaptive 3D scene representations. However, most existing 3D memory frameworks rely on static world assumptions. When objects are displaced by human activities or unobserved events, agents encounter memory-observation conflicts and often require costly geometric recomputations or inefficient global re-exploration. To address this, we propose HitMem, a hierarchical temporal 3D memory framework with a multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism. Through continuous perception, HitMem unifies semantic and spatial information into a lightweight topological graph that captures support relationships, while a temporal decay mechanism dynamically regulates memory activeness to mitigate the impact of stale representations. In addition, the multi-modal context-aware retrieval mechanism defaults to filtering candidates using integrated semantic, spatial, and temporal memory features, and activates a specialized two-stage retrieval process when object displacement is detected. This process combines spatial constraints inferred from external agent trajectories with semantic common sense grounded in class affinities, efficiently identifying high-probability candidate regions. Extensive evaluations on our constructed Dyna-THOR benchmark demonstrate that HitMem significantly improves object relocation accuracy, reduces exploration costs, and enhances task execution performance in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [143] [Accelerating Reinforcement Learning via MPC Solver-Gradient Guidance for Weights-varying MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01061)
*Baha Zarrouki,Arslan Thobani,Jasper Hoffmann,Mattia Piccinini,Rudolf Reiter,Felix Jahncke,Sébastien Gros,Davide Scaramuzza,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-RL（Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning），一种将求解器梯度作为辅助引导信号增强强化学习的方法，用于自适应调整MPC代价函数权重。该方法在保留采样闭环回报作为优化目标的同时，利用可微MPC的低方差求解器梯度提升训练稳定性和样本效率，在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台上实现了最高70.6%的样本节省和至少54%的闭环回报提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MPC中，代价函数权重决定闭环行为，但固定参数化在多变条件下往往次优。在线自适应学习策略面临偏置-方差权衡：RL优化真实闭环回报但样本效率低，而基于梯度的策略学习（GB-PL）利用可微MPC的求解器梯度计算低方差目标但存在模型失配偏置。作者希望结合两者优势，既能保持闭环回报最优性，又能利用求解器梯度提升样本效率。

Method: SG-RL框架将求解器敏感性作为辅助引导信号注入RL训练。具体地，在PPO算法中设计了四种模块化注入方式：1）演员网络更新缩放；2）策略损失项；3）优势估计；4）价值函数学习。算法保持采样闭环回报为优化目标，同时使用有界求解器梯度作为辅助引导，兼顾无偏优化和低方差导航。

Result: 在两个全尺寸自动驾驶赛车平台（含故意引入的模型失配）上验证：SG-RL能达到PPO最优闭环回报的同时节省最高70.6%的训练样本；在闭环回报上至少优于GB-PL基线54%；并且对未见环境具备零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SG-RL有效弥合了RL（无偏但样本低效）与GB-PL（低方差但有偏）之间的鸿沟，通过求解器梯度引导实现了更稳定、更高效的MPC代价权重在线自适应学习，为该方向提供了新的算法设计思路。

Abstract: In Model Predictive Control (MPC), cost-function weights shape closed-loop behavior, yet changing conditions often make fixed parametrizations suboptimal and motivate context-dependent online adaptation. Learning such policies is difficult because behavior depends implicitly on numerical MPC solutions, producing nonlinear, potentially nonsmooth, long-horizon dependencies on policy parameters. This creates a bias-variance tradeoff: Reinforcement Learning (RL) optimizes realized closed-loop return from environment samples but is sample-inefficient, whereas Gradient-Based Policy Learning (GB-PL) uses low-variance solver gradients from differentiable MPC to optimize surrogate losses on predicted trajectories but can be biased under model mismatch. We propose Solver-Gradient Guided Reinforcement Learning (SG-RL), a solver-sensitivity augmentation for RL-based online MPC cost-weight adaptation. SG-RL keeps sampled closed-loop return as the objective and uses bounded solver-derived gradients as auxiliary guidance to improve stability and sample efficiency. We instantiate SG-RL in Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) with four modular algorithms that inject solver-gradient guidance into actor-update scaling, policy loss, advantage estimation, and value-function learning. On two full-scale autonomous racing platforms with intentional model mismatch, SG-RL reaches PPO's best closed-loop return with up to 70.6% fewer samples, outperforms GB-PL baselines by at least 54% in closed-loop return, and generalizes zero-shot to unseen environments.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Depth-Map-Guided Bundle Adjustment for Correspondence-Free Multi-View Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01089)
*Yiran Zhou,Yingyu Wang,Shoudong Huang,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的分层深度图引导的捆绑调整框架，用于无特征对应的多视角点云配准，解决工业废钢切割中不锈钢光滑表面、重复结构等场景下的重建精度问题，并通过开源代码在自采工业数据集上验证了鲁棒性和低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在危险切割工作单元中，不规则钢废料的机器人加工需依赖稠密3-D测量替代人工视觉评估，重建误差会直接传播到下游尺寸估计和路径规划。现有基于特征提取和数据关联的多视角配准方法在光滑金属表面、重复结构、遮挡及部分重叠场景下易产生错误对应，导致姿态估计不准和重建失真。

Method: 提出基于自适应分层深度图引导的捆绑调整（ADM-BA）框架：将场景表示为全局2.5-D网格，每个单元自适应维护多个深度假设；原始深度观测直接投影到全局地图形成深度约束，无需显式特征对应；在存在多表面深度冲突的网格单元，采用基于softmax的层分配将每个观测关联到兼容的深度假设；构建非线性最小二乘公式联合优化传感器位姿和分层深度图，对应关系由深度图表示与投影模型隐式派生。

Result: 在自采集的工业数据集上进行了实验，该方法在具有挑战性的工业场景中实现了持续具有竞争力的重建精度，同时保持了鲁棒性和低计算成本，并开源代码实现。

Conclusion: 通过消除显式特征匹配这一瓶颈环节，自适应分层深度图表示结合捆绑调整能够在复杂工业环境中实现稳健且准确的无对应多点云配准，为废钢加工等场景提供可靠的测量基础。

Abstract: Robotic processing of irregular steel scrap requires dense 3-D measurement to replace manual visual assessment in hazardous cutting workcells. The reconstructed map is used to estimate piece dimensions, boundary geometry, feasible preheating and cutting regions, and collision-aware torch paths. The reconstruction errors therefore propagate directly to downstream measurement and planning. Existing multi-view registration methods commonly rely on feature extraction and data association to establish correspondences between views. In workcells with smooth metallic surfaces, repeated structures, occlusions, and partial overlaps, however, wrong correspondences may be established, leading to inaccurate pose estimation and distorted reconstruction. This paper presents an adaptive layered depth-map-guided bundle adjustment framework for correspondence-free multi-view point cloud registration. The scene is represented by a global 2.5-D grid, where each cell can adaptively maintain multiple depth hypotheses. Raw depth observations are directly projected into the global map to form depth constraints without explicit feature correspondences. At grid cells where multiple surfaces produce conflicting depths, a softmax-based layer assignment links each observation to compatible depth hypotheses. The resulting nonlinear least-squares formulation jointly refines sensor poses and the layered depth map, with correspondences implicitly induced by the depth-map representation and projection model. Experiments on self-collected industrial datasets show that the proposed method achieves consistently competitive reconstruction accuracy while maintaining robustness and low computational cost in challenging industrial scenarios. We release the open-source code implementation at: https://github.com/YiranZhou-Robotics/ADM-BA.git

</details>


### [145] [DNC-IMM: Early Lane-Change Intention Recognition via Neural Calibration Based on Driving Context Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01120)
*Woong-Chan Byun,Seung-Hyun Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双神经校准交互多模型（DNC-IMM）方法，利用神经网络校准状态转移概率矩阵和测量似然，在保持传统IMM概率结构与可解释性的同时提升对驾驶环境的适应性，实现换道意图的提前识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶与高级驾驶辅助系统中，提前识别换道意图对于主动决策至关重要，传统方法在适应复杂驾驶上下文方面存在不足。

Method: 提出DNC-IMM框架，使用神经网络编码驾驶上下文信息（包括目标车辆运动、与周围车辆间隙及相对速度），同时校准转移概率矩阵和测量似然；最终意图基于校准后的IMM模式后验概率判定，而非使用独立的直接分类器。

Result: 在highD数据集上，所提方法能够在车道跨越前可靠识别换道意图，在较早的2-3秒预测时域内表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: DNC-IMM在提升驾驶适应性（通过神经网络校准）与保持概率化、可解释的IMM结构之间取得了良好平衡，适合换道意图早期识别任务。

Abstract: Early recognition of lane-change intention is essential for proactive decision-making in autonomous driving and advanced driver assistance systems. This paper proposes a Dual Neural-Calibrated Interacting Multiple Model (DNC-IMM) that improves adaptability to driving context while preserving the probabilistic structure and interpretability of a conventional IMM. The proposed method encodes driving-context information, including target-vehicle motion, gaps to surrounding vehicles, and relative velocities, with a neural network that calibrates both the transition-probability matrix and measurement likelihoods. The final intention is determined from the calibrated IMM mode posterior rather than from a separate direct classifier. Experiments on the highD dataset demonstrate that the proposed method reliably recognizes lane-change intentions before lane crossing and provides particularly strong performance at the earlier 2-3 s prediction horizons.

</details>


### [146] [On Global Regulatability of Robot Manipulators by Classical PID](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01207)
*Cheng Zhao,Jingru Zhu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有可定义向量相对度（各通道相对度分量可能不同）的不确定MIMO非线性系统，提出了通道级扩展PID（EPID）控制器。每个控制输入由对应跟踪误差的比例、积分和微分项构成，最高导数阶次根据该通道的相对度选择。在适当的未知非线性增长条件下，构造了由预设初始状态界限、不确定性和参考信号确定的EPID参数可行集，证明该集合中任意参数均可保证闭环系统半全局稳定性并实现设定点调节。结果表明EPID参数可逐通道独立设计，为工程实践中（包括飞行控制系统）对强耦合不确定MIMO系统进行逐通道整定的常见做法提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PID型控制文献对MIMO非线性系统的研究有限，尤其是各通道相对度分量可能不同的系统几乎没有涉及。作者希望为这类系统提供理论保证，并为实际工程中逐通道独立设计PID参数的普遍做法提供严谨的理论支撑。

Method: 提出通道级EPID控制器：针对每个通道，依据该通道的相对度选择合适的比例、积分、微分导数阶次构造控制律。在未知非线性满足适当增长条件的前提下，基于预设初始状态界限、不确定性和参考信号构建EPID参数的可行集（admissible set），并证明该集合内任意参数组合均能保证闭环系统半全局稳定性及设定点调节。

Result: 证明了在构造的参数可行集内任意选择EPID参数，都能保证闭环系统的半全局稳定性并实现期望的设定点调节。这验证了EPID参数可以逐通道独立设计，即使在强耦合和不确定的MIMO系统（包括飞行控制系统）中亦是如此。

Conclusion: 本文为通道间相对度异构的不确定MIMO非线性系统提供了通道级EPID控制的理论框架与稳定性保证，从理论上验证了通道间逐通道独立的PID参数整定实践的合理性，为工程应用（特别是飞行控制）提供了重要理论依据。

Abstract: This paper studies a class of uncertain multi-input multi-output (MIMO) nonlinear systems using extended PID (EPID) control. We focus on systems possessing a well-defined vector relative degree whose components may vary across channels, a setting that received limited attention in the existing literature on PID-type control. We develop a channel-wise EPID controller, where each control input is constructed from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of the corresponding tracking error, and the highest derivative order is selected according to the relative degree of that channel. Under suitable growth conditions on the unknown nonlinearities, we construct an admissible set of EPID parameters specified by prescribed initial-state bounds, the uncertainties and the reference signal. We show that any parameter choice from this set guarantees semiglobal stability of the closed-loop system and achieves the desired setpoint regulation. These results indicate that the EPID parameters can be designed independently for each channel, justifying the common engineering practice of channel-by-channel tuning in strongly coupled and uncertain MIMO systems, including flight control systems.

</details>


### [147] [EmbodiedSkills: A Unified Framework for Orchestrating, Training, and Deploying VLA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01281)
*Wei Wang,Wenqiao Zhang,Yutong Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Tianwei Lin,Jinhao Mao,Zhenxuan Fan,Mingjian Gao,Yang Dai,Wentong Li,Zheqi Lv,Zheng Dong,Yingjie Niu,Jiaqi Zhu,Jun Xiao,Chao Li,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbodiedSkills框架，将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的每个技能决策视为执行提案，通过共享的可执行技能接口在单个代理回路中集成高层技能选择、受约束的低层VLA执行和动作后验证，实现闭环具身系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务需要的不只是动作预测，还需要感知、规划、执行、进度验证与恢复的协调。仅仅预测动作或生成技能决策无法保证操作在当前物理状态下有效，也无法验证执行结果是否正确。现有VLA模型缺乏此闭环能力。

Method: EmbodiedSkills将每个技能决策视为执行提案（execution proposal），运行时在执行前检查前置条件、执行后验证结果。通过固定的可执行技能接口连接高层技能选择、受限的低层VLA执行和动作后验证，形成统一代理回路。该接口保持固定，支持低层VLA策略的替换或适配而不改变代理回路；同时记录规划、执行、验证和恢复事件为结构化轨迹，为各组件提供监督并支持在线自适应。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的50个任务上平均成功率达86.20%，在LIBERO四个套件上平均成功率达97.40%。但在四个记忆依赖的RMBench任务上，仅12.5%的平均成功率。该框架为将这些策略转变为闭环具身系统提供了可训练、可检查的代理层。

Conclusion: 任务自适应的低层VLA策略在常规任务上表现出很强的执行性能，但EmbodiedSkills框架本身的价值在于提供一个可训练且可检查的代理层，将VLA策略能力转化为闭环具身系统。RMBench上的低成功率暗示框架仍需在记忆依赖的长时程任务上进行改进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models map visual observations and language instructions directly to robot actions, but long-horizon tasks require more than action prediction. An agent must coordinate perception, planning, execution, progress verification, and recovery as the physical state evolves. An action prediction or a model-generated skill decision does not, by itself, guarantee that the proposed operation is valid in the current state or that its outcome will be verified. We propose EmbodiedSkills, a unified framework that treats each skill decision as an execution proposal: the runtime checks its prerequisites before execution and verifies the outcome afterward. A shared executable-skill interface connects high-level skill selection, bounded low-level VLA execution, and post-action verification within a single agent loop. Because this interface remains fixed, low-level VLA policies can be replaced or adapted without changing the agent loop. The interface also records planning, execution, verification, and recovery events as structured trajectories, which provide supervision for individual components and can support optional online adaptation when interactive feedback is available. We instantiate EmbodiedSkills with Qwen3-VL and OpenPI/pi0.5 on RoboTwin 2.0 and LIBERO. Task-adapted low-level VLA policies achieve an average success rate of 86.20% across 50 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks and 97.40% across the four LIBERO suites. These results establish the execution performance of the task-adapted low-level VLA policies used in EmbodiedSkills. On four memory-dependent RMBench tasks, the same task-adapted execution approach achieves 12.5% average success. The framework provides a trainable and inspectable agent layer for turning these policies into closed-loop embodied systems.

</details>


### [148] [Integrating Traffic Noise Emission Modelling into Variable Speed Limit Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01339)
*Jiawen Meng,John Pravin Arockiasamy,Alexey Vinel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种噪声感知的可变限速（VSL）框架，将聚合交通状态估计与简化的CNOSSOS-EU排放指标结合，通过分级控制器动态调整限速水平。在24小时仿真评估中，该策略相比无限制交通将接收点等效声级降低2.9 dB(A)，同时保持比永久低速制度高约11.3 km/h的平均车速，证明了将环境性能指标纳入运营速度控制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路交通噪声是重要的环境挑战，但现有速度管理策略多为静态的，无法响应交通噪声排放的短期变化。尽管可变限速（VSL）系统已广泛用于安全和缓解拥堵，但交通噪声很少被视为明确的操作控制目标。

Method: 提出噪声感知VSL框架，包含三个关键部分：(1)聚合交通状态估计；(2)简化的基于CNOSSOS-EU的排放指标；(3)具有时变参考阈值的分级控制器，根据估计的排放状况动态调整离散限速等级。使用微观交通仿真进行评估，仿真基于实测高速公路数据标定，并在多个随机实现中重复验证。

Result: 24小时评估期内，自适应策略相比无限制交通条件将接收点等效声级降低2.9 dB(A)，同时平均车速比永久低速制度高约11.3 km/h。时段分析表明限速仅在排放水平接近校准阈值时选择性激活，而非强制执行恒定的中间限速。交通稳定性指标显示车速变异性比无限制运行略有增加，但制动强度显著低于均匀低速执法。

Conclusion: 结果表明将环境绩效指标纳入运营速度控制是可行的，为传统基于基础设施的噪声缓解措施提供了实用补充。

Abstract: Road traffic noise remains a major environmental challenge, yet most speed management strategies are static and do not respond to short-term variations in traffic noise emissions. Although variable speed limit (VSL) systems are widely deployed for safety and congestion mitigation, traffic noise is rarely treated as an explicit operational control objective.
  This paper proposes a noise-aware VSL framework that integrates aggregated traffic-state estimation with a simplified CNOSSOS-EU-based emission indicator. A stage-based controller with time-varying reference thresholds dynamically adjusts discrete speed-limit levels in response to estimated emission conditions. The framework is evaluated using microscopic traffic simulation calibrated with empirical motorway data and replicated across multiple stochastic realisations.
  Over a 24-hour evaluation period, the adaptive strategy reduces the receiver-based equivalent sound level by 2.9 dB(A) relative to unrestricted traffic conditions, while maintaining an average vehicle speed approximately 11.3 km/h higher than a permanently imposed low-speed regime. Period-wise analysis shows that speed reductions are activated selectively when emission levels approach calibrated targets, rather than enforcing a constant intermediate limit. Traffic stability indicators reveal moderate increases in speed variability compared with unrestricted operation, but substantially lower braking intensity than under uniform low-speed enforcement.
  These results demonstrate the feasibility of integrating environmental performance indicators into operational speed control, providing a practical complement to conventional infrastructure-based noise mitigation measures.

</details>


### [149] [Scalable Rao-Blackwellized Online Planning for High-Dimensional POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01351)
*Jiho Lee,Nisar Ahmed,Kyle Hollins Wray,Zachary Sunberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Rao-Blackwellized在线POMDP（RB-POMDP）框架，通过混合连续-离散信念表示来提升高维状态空间下的通用性。在树规划过程中对边缘化状态分量进行解析不确定性传播，减少蒙特卡洛估计的采样方差。在机器人搜索与救援任务中与FastSLAM 2.0集成验证，结果表明在同等计算预算下，该方法以显著更少的粒子和规划模拟获得更高累计奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线规划在部分可观测环境中仍面临高维状态空间的挑战。采样式POMDP求解器虽能在大规模或连续域中提供近似决策，但随着信念维度增加，基于蒙特卡洛估计的高方差导致性能显著退化，亟需降低采样方差的方法来提升高维场景下的规划性能。

Method: 扩展RB-POMDP框架，采用混合连续-离散信念表示；在基于树的规划过程中，对边缘化状态分量进行解析不确定性传播（Rao-Blackwellization），以降低价值估计中的采样诱导方差；并与FastSLAM 2.0集成用于机器人搜索与救援任务。

Result: 在机器人搜索与救援任务中，相较于纯采样方法，所提出的规划器在同等计算预算下使用显著更少的粒子和规划模拟即可获得更高的累计奖励。

Conclusion: 具有可处理充分统计量的结构化高维机器人问题可以通过RB-POMDP框架有效利用，实现计算上可行的在线决策，表明解析推理与采样结合是处理高维POMDP在线规划的有效途径。

Abstract: Online planning under uncertainty remains a fundamental challenge for robotic systems operating in partially observable environments with high-dimensional state spaces. While sampling-based POMDP solvers enable approximate decision-making in large or continuous domains, their performance degrades as belief dimensionality increases due to the high variance inherent in Monte Carlo-based estimation. In this work, we extend the Rao-Blackwellized online POMDP (RB-POMDP) framework to improve its generalizability in high-dimensional settings through hybrid continuous-discrete belief representations. By analytically propagating uncertainty associated with marginalized state components during tree-based planning, the proposed approach reduces sampling-induced variance in value estimation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of this framework in a robotic search-and-rescue task by integrating it with FastSLAM 2.0. Experimental results show that the proposed planner achieves higher cumulative rewards using significantly fewer particles and planning simulations than purely sampling-based methods under equivalent computational budgets. These results suggest that structured high-dimensional robotic problems admitting tractable sufficient statistics can be effectively leveraged within the RB-POMDP framework for computationally feasible online decision-making.

</details>


### [150] [Obstacle-Aware Autonomous Coverage and Navigation for Outdoor Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01384)
*Leonardo Gargani,Matteo Frosi,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的ROS 2架构，用于户外覆盖任务的长时间自主执行，整合了覆盖规划、鲁棒定位（双天线RTK-GNSS+EKF）和Nav2执行，并加入控制器感知的规划改进与行为树任务管理，实现在多种户外场景下93.1%-96.1%的覆盖率和可靠的自主返航充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典规划方法难以应对户外长时间覆盖任务中的多重挑战：开阔空间中的定位漂移、杂乱站点的障碍物、急转弯中的控制器可行性问题，以及能量管理所需的持久自主性。现有的单一模块方案无法同时解决这些跨层次的耦合问题。

Method: 提出统一的ROS 2架构，包含：(1) 双天线RTK-GNSS融合EKF实现位置和航向的精确估计；(2) 在成熟覆盖规划器基础上加入三种控制器感知的细化改进；(3) 基于行为树的任务管理器，协调多目标执行、分层恢复、成本感知的目标管理及自主停靠返航充电；(4) 基于Nav2的执行层。

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界试验，在多种几何形状和障碍物密度的户外区域验证了该架构，覆盖率达到计划覆盖面积的93.1%至96.1%，能够可靠完成长持续时间的户外覆盖任务。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一ROS 2覆盖栈能够可靠地完成多样户外区域的覆盖任务，同时满足定位精度、控制器可行性和能量管理等多方面要求，为户外持久自主覆盖提供了一套可落地的完整解决方案。

Abstract: Long-duration outdoor coverage with autonomous platforms remains challenging beyond classical planning: deployments face localization drift in open spaces, obstacles in cluttered sites, controller feasibility in turn-heavy maneuvers, and persistent autonomy with energy management. We propose a unified ROS 2 architecture for outdoor coverage that combines coverage planning, robust localization, and Nav2-based execution. A dual-antenna RTK-GNSS fused in an EKF keeps the robot pose, both position and heading, accurate across long missions; three controller-aware refinements are added to a mature coverage planner; a Behavior-Tree mission manager coordinates multi-goal execution, layered recovery, cost-aware goal management, and autonomous docking for return-to-charge. We validate the stack through simulation and real-world trials across multiple outdoor areas with varying geometries and obstacle densities. Overall, these results show that the proposed stack can reliably complete outdoor coverage missions across varied areas, sweeping 93.1% to 96.1% of the planned coverage area.

</details>


### [151] [Autonomous robotic bridging using distributed swarm control without inter-agent communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01394)
*Vishwaak C. Thamaraiselvan,Cody L. Lundberg,Michail Theofandis,Suhas Chelian,Nicholas R. Gans*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍SCARAB（具备群体能力的自主机器人水架桥系统），通过分布式群体控制和多模型传感，使智能体能够在无GPS和RF通信的条件下自主定位、编队并到达目标位置，实现分散式渡河操作，提高乘员生存能力并降低后勤负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的Improved Ribbon Bridge在渡河作业中存在后勤负担大、可预测性强、乘员生存风险高等问题，需要一种更灵活、分散、自主的桥梁架设方案。

Method: 采用分布式群体控制算法与多模型传感器融合技术，使无人水面车辆（USVs）实现自定位、编队形成和目标位置到达；系统无需GPS或RF通信即可工作（但在非对抗环境如民用救灾中也可使用）；通过基于物理引擎的仿真在含水流和风条件下验证系统性能。

Result: 通过物理引擎仿真验证了多架USV在存在水流和风干扰的条件下能够成功实现自主定位、编队和目标导航。

Conclusion: SCARAB技术有望使陆军实现不可预测的分散式渡河，提升乘员生存率并减小后勤足迹，同时该系统在军民两用场景均有应用潜力。

Abstract: We describe SCARAB--Swarm-Capable Autonomous Robotic Aquatic Bridging. Using distributed swarm control and multi-model sensing of agents and docking targets, agents can localize themselves, join into formations and proceed to desired target locations. Our technologies would eventually allow the Army to perform unpredictable, dispersed river crossings, enhance crew survivability, and minimize the logistics footprint compared to the current Improved Ribbon Bridge. Our methods operate without GPS or RF communications, though these can be used in non-contested environments (e.g., civilian disaster relief for flooding, etc.). We demonstrate our system via physics-engine-based simulation of several agents using unmanned surface vehicles (USVs) in the presence of currents and wind.

</details>


### [152] [Evaluating Multimodal LLMs as Generalist Vision-Language-Action Agents for Drone Control: Commanding, Approaching, Tracking and Searching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01404)
*Jaewoo Park,Minyoung Lee,Sukmin Seo,Moonbin Yim,Hyunwook Yoon,Dohoon Ryu,Daehee Kim,Myungseo Song,Jihyuk Byun,Seunggyu Chang,Taeho Kil,Jiseob Kim,Bado Lee,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DroneCATS-Agent架构与DroneCATS基准，将多模态大模型直接嵌入无人机控制回路，通过纯提示词声明完整动作空间（不微调、无函数调用），评估模型从目标逼近、动态跟踪、视野外搜索到多机编队指挥的四项核心能力。研究发现小型开源模型在导航上常优于前沿模型，但会过早或过晚声明到达而失败；决定性差距不在空间感知或导航，而在于模型维持行动协议、正确发出终止动作的纪律性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将MLLM用于无人机控制的系统逐步收窄模型的决策空间，限制了模型作为具身智能体的潜力。作者希望逆转这一趋势，把MLLM当作真正的决策主体直接投入控制回路，并系统性地评估不同规模模型在该设定下的能力边界，特别是识别在小参数边缘场景中首先失效的环节。

Method: 提出DroneCATS-Agent，一种将MLLM作为可替换组件的架构，模型全动作空间仅在提示词中声明，无微调与函数调用模式。构建DroneCATS基准，将模型视为自变量，涵盖四个能力维度：接近可见目标、跟踪移动目标、初始视野外搜索以及多无人机编队指挥。评测规模从前沿模型下探至2B参数的开源模型。

Result: 即使最简单的具身设定也远未解决。发现悖论：小型开源模型在导航进入成功半径方面往往比前沿模型更可靠，但会在过早声明到达或根本不声明到达中丢失回合。多机编队指令加剧了这一分化，小模型会盲目地在不同视角复制同一坐标。模型的视觉-语言-动作空间感知表现良好，但动作协议执行失败。

Conclusion: 区分可部署边缘模型与前沿模型的关键不在于导航能力，而在于能否维持所声明的协议并发出正确的终止动作。开放问题是在机载计算成本下缩小这一差距，获得一个既能持续规划又清楚何时完成的快速模型，DroneCATS正是为度量这一距离而设计。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are strong perceivers of images and video. We ask how far that reach extends into acting: dropping an MLLM directly into a drone's control loop, with its entire action space declared solely in the prompt. Recent systems approach this setting but increasingly narrow the model's decision-making. We widen it back. We introduce DroneCATS-Agent, an architecture where the MLLM is a swappable component, and DroneCATS, a benchmark treating the model as the independent variable. Beyond merely flying toward a pixel, our agent entrusts the model to yaw and search, deliberate when unsure, and self-declare arrival---all without fine-tuning or function-calling schemas. Evaluating frontier and open models across four core capabilities---approaching a visible target, tracking a moving one, searching outside the initial view, and commanding a multi-drone fleet---reveals that even the simplest embodied settings are far from solved. Crucially, to identify what breaks first at the edge, our roster scales down to 2B parameters. The findings expose a stark paradox: it is not the flying that fails. Small open models often navigate into the success radius more reliably than frontier models, yet lose the episode by declaring arrival prematurely or not at all. Multi-drone commanding amplifies this divide, with small models failing by blindly copying a single coordinate across distinct views. Viewed as vision-language-action agents, the models' spatial perception holds up, but their action protocol does not. What separates a deployable edge model from a frontier model is not navigation, but the discipline to sustain a declared protocol and emit the correct terminating action. The open problem is closing this gap at onboard compute costs---yielding a fast model that plans persistently and knows exactly when it is done---and DroneCATS is built to measure that distance.

</details>


### [153] [Vision-Based Leader-Follower Formation Control for Cooperative UAVs in GPS-Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01420)
*Deekshitha Angadi,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Rojesh Arunkumar Mulasa,Ravi Killamsetty,Mohamed Samshad,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于视觉的多无人机编队框架，使用前视RGB-D相机检测、跟踪并定位领航无人机，在GPS或外部定位失效时为跟随者提供相对位姿估计和编队控制备份方案。系统基于轻量级YOLO检测器与针孔相机模型融合深度信息，实现在物理仿真中的实时编队保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机系统中的协同编队要求可靠的相对感知能力，但绝对定位传感器（如GPS）可能退化或失效，导致跟随者无法维持编队和安全执行任务。需要一种不依赖绝对定位的视觉相对感知方案来保障编队控制。

Method: 采用前视RGB-D相机作为感知输入；使用在专用无人机数据集上训练的轻量级YOLO检测器进行实时领航无人机目标检测与边界框预测；将边界框与深度信息通过针孔相机模型融合，估计领航者的相对位姿；相对位姿估计驱动领航-跟随位置控制器，并可在GPS失效时作为备份；整个框架以ROS节点实现，并在基于XTDrone的物理仿真环境中加入传感器噪声与通信丢包进行验证。

Result: 评估了检测精度、运行时间和编队保持误差。在标称条件下及定位传感器模拟故障下，框架均能维持稳定的领航-跟随编队，且计算开销合理。

Conclusion: 该视觉编队框架在物理仿真中展现出稳定的编队保持能力和较低的计算负担，为将视觉协同编队控制拓展到真实多无人机系统提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: Cooperation in multi-UAV systems requires reliable relative perception so that follower vehicles can maintain formation and continue their mission safely even when absolute positioning sensors degrade or fail. This paper presents a vision-based cooperative formation framework running on a follower UAV that uses a front-facing RGB-D camera to detect, track, and localize a leader UAV in real-time. A lightweight YOLO-based detector is trained on a dedicated drone dataset and deployed onboard to predict leader bounding boxes, which are then fused with depth information via a pinhole camera model to estimate the leader's relative pose. These estimates provide a leader-follower position controller and can also be used as a backup when GPS or external localization is unavailable. This framework is implemented as a set of ROS nodes and evaluated in a physics-based multi-UAV simulation built on XTDrone, with sensor noise and communication dropouts. We evaluate detection accuracy, runtime, and formation-keeping error under nominal conditions and under simulated failures of the positioning sensors. The results show that the proposed framework maintains stable leader-follower formations with reasonable computational cost and provides a practical basis for extending vision-based cooperative formation control to real-world multi-UAV systems.

</details>


### [154] [Does Imitation Learning Preserve Temporal Robustness in Dexterous Manipulation? An Expert-Learner Comparison Across Task Execution Speeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01453)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了模仿学习策略在名义速度下与专家表现一致，但在演示覆盖的速度范围内鲁棒性显著下降。以包裹存储任务(ParcelStow)为基准，ACT策略在最大演示速度下成功率从100%降至53%，而专家仅降至84%，插入错位是主要失败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的灵巧操作模仿学习评估主要关注场景、物体或指令变化下的鲁棒性，却很少考察策略在不同执行速度下的表现。这引出一个关键问题：学习者在时间维度上相对所模仿的专家保留了多少鲁棒性？论文旨在通过在相同任务条件下对比专家与学习者在不同加速因子下的表现来填补这一空白。

Method: 论文在ParcelStow任务中（机器人获取、重新定向并插入包裹的接触密集型任务）构造评估框架。演示覆盖包裹获取后各操作阶段的加速范围。使用脚本化专家与基于这些演示训练的动作分块Transformer(ACT)策略进行对比，在相同任务条件、初始条件采样和加速因子下评估两者成功率，并辅以阶段级（stage-level）分析和不同参数初始化策略的对比。

Result: 名义速度下专家与ACT均达到100%成功率，但在演示速度范围内出现分化：专家最大加速下成功率84%，ACT仅53%。两个不同初始化的ACT策略从名义速度到最大演示速度的下降幅度分别为34和48个百分点，而专家仅下降16个百分点。阶段分析表明，ACT在最大速度下47次失败中有35次是插入错位。在相对运动交接下，所有ACT获取均能保持包裹完成空中重定向与转移，但仅64%完成整体任务（专家获取后为95%）。所有策略和速度下，414次缺乏力闭合的获取均未能完成任务。

Conclusion: 名义速度下任务成功等价并不意味着学习者在执行速度范围内保持了专家的表现。时间鲁棒性应作为模仿学习策略评估的关键维度，且对接触密集型任务尤为突出。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies learned by imitation are typically evaluated for robustness to variation in scenes, objects, or instructions, but their performance across task execution speeds is less often examined. This leaves open how much temporal robustness a learner retains relative to the expert it imitates. We compare an expert and learner under the same task conditions, initial-condition draws, and speedup factors. We instantiate the evaluation in ParcelStow, a contact-rich task in which the robot acquires, reorients, and inserts a parcel. The demonstrations span the speedup range for the manipulation phases after parcel acquisition. A scripted expert and an Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT) policy trained from the expert's demonstrations both achieve 100 percent task success at nominal speed. Their success rates diverge within the demonstrated range: at its maximum, expert success is 84 percent and ACT success is 53 percent. Two ACT policies with different parameter initializations show similar degradation, decreasing by 34 and 48 percentage points from nominal speed to the maximum demonstrated speed, compared with 16 points for the expert. Stage-level analysis shows that 35 of ACT's 47 failures at the maximum demonstrated speed are insertion misalignments. Under the relative-motion handoff, every ACT acquisition retains the parcel through reorientation and transfer in free space, but only 64 percent complete the overall task, compared with 95 percent after expert acquisition. Across all evaluated policies and speeds, none of the 414 acquisitions without force closure completes the task. Equal nominal task success therefore does not imply preservation of expert performance across execution speeds. Code, data, and evaluation scripts are available at https://github.com/coenwerem/parcelstow.

</details>


### [155] [A System for Fast, Resilient, and Adaptable Loco-Manipulation Behaviors on Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01518)
*Duncan Calvert,Luigi Penco,Dexton Anderson,Tomasz Bialek,Arghya Chatterjee,Beomyeong Park,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向人形机器人的运行时可编辑行为编排与运行系统，结合对象中心的行为模板、树状逻辑结构以及运行时可编辑的感知模块，并通过全身控制器执行动作原语。系统在Unitree H1-2和Alex上演示了六种任务变体，推门穿越用时34秒，在人为干扰下按颜色分拣6个球用时45秒，新行为从零创建和已有行为适应可在数小时内完成，性能与近期学习系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在为人类设计的物理空间中执行复杂任务时，需要协调运动、全身动作、感知、接触和操作员监督。现有方法在这些方面缺乏统一的、可快速修改的行为编排框架，导致行为创建和适应性调整缓慢，难以兼顾能力、速度和可靠性。

Method: 提出一种机器人本地的运行时可编辑行为编排与运行系统。核心架构包括：(1) 对象中心的Affordance Templates与提供组织和逻辑的树结构相结合；(2) 通过行为场景和原始场景动作实现运行时可编辑的感知；(3) 操作员界面与机器人持续同步，支持运行时编排、监控和修复；(4) 动作原语通过支持并发身体运动和行走的全身控制器执行。

Result: 在Unitree H1-2和Alex上演示了六种任务变体。推门穿越任务用时34秒，在人为干扰下按颜色分拣六个球用时45秒。计时编排实验表明新操控-移动行为可从零开始数小时内创建，已有行为也可在数小时内完成改编。与文献比较，方法性能与近期学习系统具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 行为架构是实现人形机器人能力、速度和可靠性的关键推动因素，运行时可编辑性能够支持行为的快速创建、适应、扩展和组合。该系统展示了结构化行为编排方法在全身操控任务中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: There is tremendous value in humanoid robots taking on physically demanding, hazardous, and repetitive work in spaces built for humans. However, a useful robot for these spaces must coordinate locomotion, whole-body motion, perception, contact, and operator supervision. We present a robot-local, runtime-editable behavior authoring and runtime system that addresses these challenges. We argue that behavior architecture can be a primary enabler of capability, speed, and reliability, and that runtime editability enables fast behavior creation, adaptation, extension, and combination.
  Our behavior architecture combines object-centric Affordance Templates, a tree structure that provides organization and logic, and runtime-editable perception through a behavior scene and primitive scene actions. Our operator interface remains continuously synchronized to the robot for runtime authoring, monitoring, and repair. Action primitives execute through a whole-body controller that supports concurrent body motions and walking.
  Demonstrations of our system cover six task variants on Unitree H1-2 and Alex. We execute a push door traversal in 34 seconds and sort six balls by color in 45 seconds under human disturbance. Timed authoring sessions show scratch creation of new loco-manipulation behaviors and adaptation of existing ones in hours. Comparison against the literature finds our approach to be competitive with recent learned systems.

</details>


### [156] [SG-AMP: Scene-Graph-Guided Active Perception and Semantics-Aware Motion Planning for Pepper Plants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01579)
*Rohit Menon,Shiva Rudra Lolla,Niklas Mueller-Goldingen,Gokul Chenchani,Ribana Roscher,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-AMP系统，将鲁棒深度补全（带输入条件不确定性）、持久全景建图、植物场景图推理与语义感知的主动视角运动规划相结合，用于青椒果实检测与采摘场景。系统不仅检查不确定的已观测区域，还通过场景图显式假设未观测的辣椒-果柄连接关系，并引导近距感知。在青椒数据上感知网络达到55.27%语义mIoU、38.67% PQ和40.62mm深度RMSE，输入条件不确定性使NYUv2的NLL从-1.6518提升至-1.6925，AUSE从0.0102降至0.0087。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在农业机器人（青椒采摘）场景中，需要精准感知果实、果柄等关键结构，但仅观测当前可见区域不够，还需对未观测的胡椒-果柄连接关系进行推理，并主动规划视角以补充感知信息，同时考虑不同类别（如保护果实与可穿越叶片）的运动成本差异。

Method: 提出SG-AMP框架，包含四个关键组件：(1) 带输入条件不确定性的鲁棒深度补全网络；(2) 持久全景建图模块；(3) 植物场景图推理，用于显式假设未观测区域的辣椒-果柄连接；(4) 语义感知的主动视角-运动规划。视点选择基于期望信息增益，运动代价根据类别区分（受保护的辣椒、果柄、茎杆与可条件性穿越的叶片）。

Result: 在青椒数据上，感知网络实现55.27%语义mIoU、38.67%全景质量PQ和40.62mm深度RMSE。输入条件不确定性模块使NYUv2上的NLL从-1.6518提升至-1.6925，AUSE从0.0102降至0.0087，表明不确定性校准效果显著改善。

Conclusion: SG-AMP通过场景图驱动的主动感知策略，有效解决了农业机器人中对未观测关键结构（胡椒-果柄连接）的推理与观测问题，结合深度补全不确定性校准与类别感知运动规划，为复杂植物环境中的精准采摘感知提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: We present SG-AMP, integrating robust depth completion with input-conditioned uncertainty, persistent panoptic mapping, plant scene-graph reasoning, and semantics-aware active view-motion planning. Beyond inspecting uncertain observed regions, the scene graph explicitly hypothesizes unobserved pepper--peduncle attachments and directs close-range sensing toward them. Candidate views are selected according to expected information gain, while class-dependent motion costs distinguish protected peppers, peduncles, and stems from conditionally traversable foliage. On pepper data, the perception network achieves $55.27\%$ semantic mIoU, $38.67\%$ PQ, and $40.62\,\mathrm{mm}$ depth RMSE, while input-conditioned uncertainty improves NYUv2 NLL from $-1.6518$ to $-1.6925$ and AUSE from $0.0102$ to $0.0087$.

</details>


### [157] [Facet-0: A Robotic Foundation Model for Contact-Rich Precise Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01596)
*Haoyuan Deng,Haichao Liu,Wenkai Guo,Yuan Ling,Zaijia Yang,Yuanjiang Xue,Haosheng Sun,Liangzi Wang,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Facet-0，一个面向亚毫米级精细装配任务的机器人基础模型。它通过联合动作-扳手(wrench)提议统一多模态表示学习与强化学习(RL)后训练，利用流匹配生成动作块及其预期腕部扳手力/扭矩反馈轮廓，并通过分布式的动作-扳手评判器(Action-Wrench Critic)和接触选择性信用分配优化策略。在ManuFacet-1K数据集（1000小时力同步多体制语料）上训练，该模型在五个亚毫米计算机装配任务上达到82%的平均成功率（基线最强为15%），定位精度0.5mm，命令延迟50ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的机器人装配需要在亚毫米公差下同时具备空间精度、柔顺交互能力和对接触故障的鲁棒性。现有的机器人基础模型通常缺乏对接触后果(contact consequence)的预测与价值评估能力，难以应对精密装配中接触状态多变、失败模式多样的问题。作者旨在构建一个能够预测并评估自身动作所引起的接触后果的机器人基础模型。

Method: Facet-0采用联合动作-扳手提议框架：(1) 将因果扳手历史与视觉-语言语义和运动学状态对齐，实现多模态表示学习；(2) 使用流匹配(flow matching)生成动作块以及该动作未来预期诱发的腕部扳手力/力矩分布；(3) 在部署滚动训练中引入分布式的Action-Wrench Critic，用于区分任务进度相似但接触后果不同的动作；(4) 设计相位感知奖励(phase-aware rewards)和接触选择性信用分配机制，将策略改进聚焦于关键交互步骤；(5) 采用轻量级有界actor复用冻结表示进行机上自适应，RL在可执行的笛卡尔动作上定义，辅助扳手头保持预测性、非指令性的动作-接触耦合。

Result: Facet-0在ManuFacet-1K数据集（涵盖三个实体和多制造单元的1000小时力同步语料）上训练后，在五个亚毫米计算机装配任务上达到82%的平均成功率，相比最强基线（仅15%）有显著提升，同时实现了0.5mm的装配定位精度和50ms的命令延迟。

Conclusion: Facet-0验证了将力/力矩接触信息（扳手）显式纳入机器人基础模型的动作生成与价值评估闭环中的有效性。通过动作-扳手联合提议、接触感知的批评器与有界适配机制，该模型在亚毫米精度装配中展现出远超基线的性能和实时性，为精密制造领域的高鲁棒性自主装配奠定了新范式。

Abstract: Real-world robotic assembly at sub-millimeter tolerances demands spatial precision, compliant interaction, and robustness to contact failures. We present Facet-0, a robotic foundation model that predicts and values the contact consequences of its actions. Facet-0 unifies multimodal representation learning and reinforcement learning (RL) post-training around a joint action-wrench proposal: a causal wrench history is aligned with vision-language semantics and kinematic state, and flow matching generates each action chunk together with the future wrist-wrench profile it is expected to induce. Deployment rollouts train a distributional Action-Wrench Critic to distinguish motions with similar task progress but different contact outcomes, while phase-aware rewards and contact-selective credit concentrate policy improvement on decisive interactions. To accommodate part-specific dynamics, a lightweight bounded actor reuses the frozen representation for on-robot adaptation; RL remains defined over executable Cartesian actions, while an auxiliary wrench head preserves predictive, non-commanded action-contact coupling. Trained on ManuFacet-1K, a 1,000-hour force-synchronized corpus spanning three embodiments and multiple manufacturing cells, the bounded task-adapted system reaches 82% mean success on five sub-millimeter computer-assembly tasks, compared with 15% for the strongest baseline, with 0.5 mm placement accuracy and 50 ms command latency.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyperWorld，一个关于文本世界模型中状态序列化结构影响的受控研究。通过在原始观测与三种符号序列化（独立句子、成对三元组、以实体为中心的hyperedge单元）之间进行系统比较，发现hyperedge序列化在中小规模模型（0.5B-1.5B）和分布外场景下表现最佳，能显著提升世界模型的效果预测与可行性判断能力，并在下游贪心规划中获得最高的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体需要学习文本环境中的符号动作效果以进行规划，但现有研究忽视了状态描述序列化结构对世界模型学习的影响。该工作希望填补这一空白，探索更高阶的状态组织方式能否作为有效的归纳偏置。

Method: 设计HyperWorld受控实验：将同一ground-truth状态分别表示为原始观测和三种符号序列化形式——独立句子、成对三元组、实体中心的hyperedge单元。所有变体使用相同的训练目标：给定状态和动作，预测符号效果或判定动作不可行。在多种模型规模、数据预算以及分布内/分布外测试世界下进行比较评估。

Result: hyperedge序列化在0.5B-1.5B模型和分布偏移条件下带来最明显的收益；更大模型能缩小差距。在分布内的精确匹配任务上，成对三元组可与hyperedge相当或略优，但hyperedge在分布外事实F1上表现最强，且在小到中等规模下实现了可行性检测与效果预测之间最佳的权衡。在贪心规划下游任务中，hyperedge世界模型取得了最高成功率。

Conclusion: 更高阶的状态组织（hyperedge序列化）是一种简单但有效的归纳偏置，尤其当模型容量有限或测试环境与训练环境存在差异时，能显著提升符号世界模型的学习效果。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [159] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE，一个用于生成式机器遗忘中"干扰"（interference）系统化研究的正式方法论框架，提供任务定义、指标和报告模板，而非提出新基准或新算法，并附带开源实现和可视化界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器遗忘虽发展迅速，但未被遗忘的语义相关概念遭受的意外退化（即干扰）现象尚未被充分刻画，且现有评估方式不一致。需要一个正式、可复现的方法论来系统化研究干扰问题。

Method: I-CARE形式化定义了任务、评估指标和结果报告模板，将干扰作为生成式遗忘中的一等公民研究对象。该方法论设计为独立于特定模型和遗忘算法演进，并用现有先进算法和常用数据集进行了可行性演示。

Result: 演示结果显示I-CARE能够在多种遗忘设置下开展有意义的干扰模式分析，验证了该框架的实用适用性。同时提供了开源软件实现和基于web的图形界面供无编程背景的研究者探索。

Conclusion: I-CARE通过提供系统化、可复现且对未来模型和算法演进保持有效的方法论，填补了生成式机器遗忘中干扰评估标准缺失的空白，实现了长期科学洞见与瞬时经验结果的解耦。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [160] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个有限时域、多物品、容量受限的批量生产计划问题（lot-sizing problem），其中需求量是确定性的，但需求到达时期是随机的。每个需求在已知时间窗口内恰好发生一次，且必须在其截止日期前满足。该问题被建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），并提出遗传算法（GA）在状态反馈策略空间上搜索求解。在330个基准实例上，GA与精确随机解的平均最优性差距约3.44%；在90个难解实例中，GA保持在5%最优性差距阈值以内，平均优化速度提升6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的批量生产计划通常假设需求到达时间是确定性的，但在许多实际供应链中，需求到达时期本身具有不确定性。本文的核心动机是解决确定性需求量但随机到达时期下的多物品容量受限批量生产问题，量化随机时序对问题计算复杂性的影响，并提出可在实际硬件上求解的高效算法。

Method: (1) 在需求层面建立生产和分配决策模型，能够刻画容量竞争、需求特定延期交付和分配依赖的库存动态。(2) 将随机问题形式化为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），包含状态空间、可行行动、转移核和单期成本函数。(3) 设计确定性对照实验：将每个到达分布替换为最可能的到达时期，以隔离随机时序的计算效应。(4) 提出遗传算法（GA），在可行状态反馈政策空间上搜索，并在DTMDP转移模型下精确评估每个政策。(5) 对无法精确求解的实例，采用经验Bellman时间回归估计缺失的精确求解时间并外推GA的预期加速比。

Result: (1) 随机时序显著增加了状态数量、转移数量、求解时间和内存压力（通过与确定性对照比较得出）。(2) 在330个基准实例上，GA与可获得的精确随机解平均最优性差距约3.44%。(3) 在90个困难测试实例中，GA保持在5%最优性差距阈值内，平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。(4) 对硬件上无法精确求解的实例，通过经验Bellman时间回归估算了缺失的精确求解耗时并外推了GA的预期加速。

Conclusion: 本文表明，需求到达时期的随机性会显著增加批量生产问题的计算复杂度，简单的确定性近似（取最可能到达时期）不足以捕捉这种复杂性。所提出的遗传算法在质量（接近最优）和效率（显著加速）之间取得了良好的平衡，是解决随机时序容量受限批量生产问题的有效方法。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [161] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于累积轮次的诈骗风险评估框架（cumulative turn-based risk assessment），该框架在对话逐步展开的过程中增量聚合对话轮次并在每一步重新评估风险，用于检测针对老年人的多轮金融诈骗（通过电子邮件、短信和电话等渠道）。作者构建了一个多轮对话数据集（涵盖投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景，每段对话含2到8轮，并在每个累积阶段标注定性风险等级、连续风险分数、解释性理由和安全建议），并在此数据集上微调评测了四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3）。实验结果表明，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在与其参数规模相比取得了更强的轮次感知风险估计性能，展现了紧凑语言模型在资源受限和隐私保护的端侧设备上实现渐进式诈骗防护的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗正越来越多地通过多轮次的文本和语音渠道（如电子邮件、短信和电话）展开，风险信号逐步累积出现在对话的不同轮次中。现有的检测方法难以在资源受限的部署场景下对动态演进的对话进行持续的风险监控，因此需要一种能逐轮更新风险评估的框架。

Method: 提出累积轮次风险评估框架，逐轮增量聚合对话内容并在每一轮重新估算风险级别；构建了涵盖投资、慈善和技术支持场景的多轮对话数据集（2-8轮），每个累积阶段均标注风险等级、连续风险分数、解释理由和安全建议；基于统一训练框架微调四个小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3），使其能捕捉欺诈语言线索和跨轮升级模式，同时保持紧凑模型架构以适配移动端和资源受限环境。

Result: 微调后的小型语言模型能够有效捕捉欺诈相关的语言线索和跨轮次的诈骗升级模式；在评测的模型中，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在与其参数量规模相比展现了更强的轮次感知风险评估性能。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模能够支持部署场景下的渐进式诈骗风险评估；紧凑型语言模型在隐私保护和端侧部署的诈骗防护方面具有潜力，为资源受限环境下的持续对话监控提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [162] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196个依赖工具调用、64轮、携带4个32位字）来干净地测试长程任务中的状态追踪能力，而非混淆指令理解等因素。gpt-oss-120b在温度0下可于多数完成运行中成功携带状态并返回正确摘要；即使将所有基础工具替换为LLM（驱动+工人）也能成功，关键影响因素是保留模型推理上下文和对工人进行投票以消除模算术错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM评估中，误差随步数级联放大，而现有agentic基准没有隔离状态追踪难度与指令理解，缺少控制组，易受捷径（如幻觉最终答案）影响，无法解释长运行失败的原因。LLM能否跨多个工具调用精确携带中间状态本身尚未被证明。

Method: 设计一个干净的测试：让模型逐步计算MD5哈希（RFC 1321），共196个依赖工具调用、64轮，模型需在上下文中携带(a,b,c,d)四个32位字。通过从零实现MD5将每次调用对齐到真实trace并可逐位检查摘要，隔离纯簿记（状态携带）失败。最强设置下用第二个LLM替换每个基础工具（驱动者+工人架构），无精确算术oracle参与。对比有无保留模型推理上下文、以及是否对思考型工人进行投票。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（MoE，~5.5B活跃参数/词元），温度0，短固定提示，在大多数完成运行中携带全部196次调用状态并返回正确摘要。即使无算术oracle、全部用LLM工人执行（含投票+思考），也能成功完成全哈希计算。残差失败可通过来源分类，区分状态携带、算术和服务层面的错误。

Conclusion: LLM（具体为gpt-oss-120b）在纯簿记式长程状态追踪任务中可以取得显著成功率，证明端到端失败并非必然源于状态携带。成功的关键因素（保留推理上下文、对工人投票）不改变权重即可提升表现，且失败可被细粒度定位到具体环节，为长程Agent评估提供了更清晰的隔离范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [163] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenAgentFlow 是一个基于控制平面/动作平面架构的AI代理安全系统，在动作提交边界处统一执行安全策略，支持跨GUI、API、工具调用和LLM调用的统一AgentEvent流治理，并允许策略动态更新而无需修改代理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的AI代理正从独立助手演化为异构系统，多个代理在同一环境中共存，安全性成为系统级动作治理问题。现有防护手段对prompt、工具调用、GUI动作等各自独立，导致执行碎片化，难以捕捉跨多步动作流中的隐蔽风险，且缺乏审计支持和策略演进能力。

Method: 提出控制平面/动作平面（control-plane/action-plane）双层架构。将待执行的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用和LLM生成调用统一规范化为AgentEvent流，通过共享的前执行策略执行点（Policy Enforcement Point）路由每个事件。控制平面维护来源追踪、会话状态、审计记录和可更新策略，使新规则可以在不改动代理、提示词、模型或执行路径的情况下生效。在Android上进行实例化验证。

Result: 在300例动作事件基准上达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；在30例动态策略测试中，安装新规则后有27例行为符合预期；在98条来自100例Android模拟器套件的追踪案例中达到90.8%原始准确率和92.9%追踪调整通过率（覆盖GUI、API和LLM规划案例）。

Conclusion: OpenAgentFlow为异构AI代理集群提供了实用的共享执行边界，在保持高准确率和攻击拦截的同时，实现了统一策略治理与动态更新能力。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [164] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAFFOLD，一个大规模计算机科学论文图形结构化数据集，包含图、标题、上下文、问答对和思维链推理轨迹，用于训练视觉语言模型理解论文中的图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文高度依赖图形（架构图、系统流程图、管线示意图），这些图形通常比文字承载更多信息。然而，目前没有公开数据集将这类图形与标题、上下文、问题、答案及逐步推理配对，而这正是训练视觉语言模型理解这些图形所必需的。

Method: 从arXiv计算机科学论文中构建（图像、标题、上下文、问答、思维链）五元组数据集。使用版面检测和PDF解析进行数据准备，并采用AI辅助问题生成步骤。提供了三种规模的数据集：SCAFFOLD-157K（3,058篇论文，29,887个图形，157,387对）、SCAFFOLD-37K（36,797对）和SCAFFOLD-12K（12,000对），后者用于基线实验。

Result: 构建了三种规模的数据集（157K/37K/12K），并基于SCAFFOLD-12K在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct模型上进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD填补了计算机科学图形问答数据集缺失的空白，为训练视觉语言模型理解论文图表提供基础，基线实验结果验证了数据集的可使用性。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [165] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UI-Venus-2是一个通用的多模态GUI基础智能体，通过统一闭环推理-行动框架跨移动、网页和桌面环境运作。该工作在三方面联合扩展：环境覆盖（170+多语言移动应用及原生桌面OS）、任务构建（基于深度研究的函数级指令生成）、以及强化学习验证（基于视觉关键点和多模型投票的轨迹级与样本级评估器），并集成安全感知机制确保关键操作的受控执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态GUI智能体虽有潜力实现数字任务自动化，但从benchmark导向模型转向可靠的真实世界应用仍面临环境覆盖有限、任务构建脆弱以及奖励验证不可靠三大挑战。

Method: 提出UI-Venus-2，采用统一闭环"推理-行动"框架，联合扩展三个关键维度：(1) 环境维度——扩展到170+多语言移动应用和原生桌面OS；(2) 任务维度——使用深度研究管道生成基于功能定位的指令；(3) 验证维度——引入轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合视觉关键点与多模型投票机制，为强化学习训练提供可靠奖励信号；同时集成安全感知机制确保后果性行动的受控执行。

Result: UI-Venus-2作为能力强、高效且开源的基础GUI智能体，在扩展环境覆盖、增强任务构建可靠性和强化学习信号验证方面均取得进展，推动了从benchmark导向向真实世界可部署应用的转变。

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2通过在环境、任务和验证三个维度的联合扩展，为构建更具泛化性、可验证性和自我反思能力的真实世界GUI智能体提供了开源基础，推进了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [166] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER是一个多智能体系统，以"桥"（将问题从一个数学领域迁移到另一个领域）作为搜索单元，用于自动证明/反驳数学猜想。系统在120个近期猜想上评估，产出10个证明、3个反例和45个部分结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学界使用不同的对象、不变量和工具工作，将问题跨领域迁移成本高昂且经常被跳过。作者旨在系统化这一"桥接"过程，使跨域数学推理成为可搜索的自动化单元。

Method: EULER围绕固定猜想并行运行直接、相邻领域和远距离领域三条推理路线进行竞争；桥只有在其提供源表示无法执行的操作且目标侧证据通过检查过的蕴含关系返回原陈述时才保留预算。六个有序压力测试在昂贵搜索开始前拒绝无效的桥。

Result: 在120个选自Journal of Combinatorial Theory Series A近期作者论文的冻结、去污染猜想上，EULER产出10个证明、3个反例和45个带范围的局部结果。消融实验显示：桥专用压力测试将错误结论从9降至3；桥材料与目标原生操作结合产生+4.2的正交互效应（单独任一因素都无法产生）；领域距离不能可靠预测成功，而可执行操作增益和有效返回才是关键预测因子。

Conclusion: EULER表明以"桥"为搜索单元的跨域数学推理是有效的。领域距离并非成功的关键，重要的是桥是否带来源表示无法执行的操作增益以及目标侧证据能否通过蕴含关系有效返回原问题陈述。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [167] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过部分线性模型下的蒙特卡洛模拟，比较了OLS、GAMs、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost等估计器的预测误差与因果估计性能（偏差、RMSE、置信区间覆盖率）之间的关系。结果表明，预测误差并不能始终作为因果估计质量的可靠指标，联合误差度量与因果偏差的关联也较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断中常使用预测误差来评估干扰函数（nuisance function）估计器的好坏，但预测误差与因果估计器实际性能之间的关系在不同性能度量下可能存在差异，这一问题的系统性研究尚不充分。

Method: 在部分线性模型框架下开展蒙特卡洛模拟，比较四种方法：普通最小二乘（OLS）、广义加性模型（GAMs）、XGBoost，以及使用XGBoost作为基学习器的双机器学习（DML-XGBoost）。评估干扰函数预测误差、偏差、RMSE和95%置信区间覆盖率，并检验基于暴露与结局干扰函数估计误差之绝对交叉乘积的联合误差度量。

Result: 在模拟设置中，XGBoost在非oracle方法中RMSE最低；DML-XGBoost通常提供更好的置信区间覆盖率。预测误差在各方法与设置间并未一致地追踪因果偏差，点估计性能最佳的方法不一定具有最好的置信区间覆盖率。联合误差度量与因果偏差的关联较弱，不能作为因果性能的独立有效指标。

Conclusion: 预测误差适用于评估干扰函数的估计质量，但不应将其直接视为因果估计器质量的衡量标准。因果估计的点估计性能与推断性能（置信区间覆盖）可能由不同的因素驱动，需要分别关注。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [168] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调BioBERT预训练生物医学语言模型的疟疾文献生物医学信息抽取方法，通过大规模语料预处理、临床实体标注和监督学习微调，在命名实体识别任务上显著优于其他编码与机器学习算法，并发布了人工标注数据集供社区使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾仍是重大全球健康负担，其文献量庞大且不断增长，从海量科学文献中抽取关键生物医学信息（如流行病学、分子机制、治疗干预相关实体）极具挑战，需要创新的自动化方法来解决。

Method: 首先从大量疟疾科学文献中筛选并进行预处理，对其中的临床意义实体进行人工标注；然后利用BioBERT（先进预训练生物医学语言模型）将文本编码为上下文感知表示；最后通过领域特定标注和监督学习对模型进行微调，以增强其生物医学命名实体抽取能力。

Result: 大量实验以及与不同编码方法和机器学习算法的对比表明，该方法的精确率（precision）、召回率（recall）和准确率（accuracy）均显著优于现有方法。此外，作者还发布了用于实体与关系抽取的人工标注数据集。

Conclusion: 微调后的BioBERT预训练语言模型在疟疾领域生物医学信息抽取任务中表现出色，所发布的人工标注数据集可支持其他健康信息学研究者训练更先进的疟疾信息抽取模型，推动医疗信息学相关研究。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease. The proposed methodology selects and preprocesses a large corpus of scientific articles on malaria, and then annotates them with entities of clinical significance. It then leverages BioBERT, a state-of-the-art pre-trained language model, to encode the textual data into context-aware representations. We fine-tune the model using domain-specific annotations and supervised learning to enhance its ability to extract relevant biomedical named entities. Extensive experiments and comparisons with different encoding and machine learning algorithms show that the proposed approach significantly outperforms them in precision, recall, and accuracy. We also publish our human-labeled dataset for entity and relation extraction to enable other health informatics researchers to train advanced models for malaria information extraction.

</details>


### [169] [AI Morbidity and Mortality: A Framework for Clinical AI Failure Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00076)
*Paulius Mui,Dean F. Sittig,Steve Labkoff,Sanjay Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI M&M框架——一种结构化的、无责制（blameless）的病例回顾框架，用于对临床AI失败案例进行基于病例的审察。该框架整合标准化病例收集、证据保全与调查级重建、工具回路归因及纠正行动追踪，每个事件按"触发-机制-临床路径-纠正行动"四个相互关联的维度分类。通过五个门诊用药和临床决策支持案例验证，两名评审者在全部20个轴级分类上达成一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床人工智能已越来越多地嵌入真实诊疗环境，但现有安全机制（如聚合模型监控和传统患者安全报告）都不足以重建和学习单个AI相关错误及险兆事件，无法解释风险如何在AI系统、临床医生、工作流程和机构控制之间的交互中产生。

Method: 提出AI M&M框架，包含四个组成部分：标准化案例收集、证据保全与调查级重建、工具回路（tool-in-loop）归因、纠正行动跟踪。每个事件按照四个相互关联的维度进行分类：触发条件（Trigger）—机制（Mechanism）—临床路径（Clinical Pathway）—纠正行动（Corrective Action），将暴露脆弱性的条件与产生风险的过程、对护理的后果以及分配的补救措施分离开来。使用五个说明性门诊用药和临床决策支持案例进行验证，由两名临床评审者独立应用全部四个分类轴。

Result: 两名临床评审者独立对五个案例应用全部四个分类轴，在20个轴级分类上达成完全一致（agreement across all 20 axis-level classifications）。

Conclusion: AI M&M旨在补充而非取代模型监控、患者安全报告和监管监督，通过将工作流中个体AI失败转化为可执行的机构学习。需要在跨机构、AI系统和临床环境中进行前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Clinical artificial intelligence is increasingly embedded in real-world care, yet existing safety mechanisms are poorly suited to reconstructing and learning from individual AI-related errors and near-misses. Aggregate model monitoring can identify performance changes, and traditional patient safety reporting can capture adverse events, but neither is designed to explain how risk emerges across the interaction among AI systems, clinicians, workflows, and institutional controls. We propose AI Morbidity and Mortality (AI M&M), a structured, blameless framework for case-based review of clinical AI failures. The framework combines standardized case intake, evidence preservation and investigator-level reconstruction, tool-in-loop attribution, and corrective-action tracking. Each event is classified across four linked dimensions: Trigger - Mechanism - Clinical Pathway - Corrective Action, separating the condition that exposed a vulnerability from the process that produced risk, its consequence for care, and the remediation assigned. We demonstrate the framework using five illustrative outpatient medication and clinical decision-support cases; two clinician reviewers independently applied all four classification axes and reached agreement across all 20 axis-level classifications. AI M&M is intended to complement, rather than replace, model monitoring, patient safety reporting, and regulatory oversight by converting individual AI-in-workflow failures into actionable institutional learning. Prospective evaluation across institutions, AI systems, and clinical settings is needed.

</details>


### [170] [Different representation learning objectives recover distinct latent structures from the same psychometric data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00100)
*Cong Cao,Tassos C. Kyriakides,Pambos Vrasidas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过教师-儿童配对数据（757对）比较了不同表征学习目标（PCA、对比学习和多任务学习）在心理测量问卷中恢复潜在组织结构的差异，发现对比目标显著提升教师-儿童检索性能但损害行为表型结构的保留，而多任务目标能部分恢复行为组织但牺牲检索性能，表明不同评估维度对应不同的潜在组织形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理测量问卷包含丰富的条目级信息，但不同表征学习目标是否恢复相同的潜在组织（latent organization）尚不清楚。作者希望探究跨信息源（教师与儿童）对应关系与行为表型结构是否是同一类潜在组织形式，以及表征学习目标的选择如何影响所恢复的潜在结构。

Method: 使用塞浦路斯ProW学前试验基线数据中757对匹配的教师-儿童样本。基于儿童SDQ、ASBI和CBRS条目响应，通过主成分分析和聚类方法刻画行为结构，得到四种行为表型。采用对比学习目标、基于PCA的表征以及一个联合优化对齐与行为预测的多任务目标进行表征学习比较。

Result: 对比目标将教师-儿童检索的Top-1准确率从0.13%提升至7.27%，Top-10准确率从1.98%提升至56.14%，但对比表征对行为表型结构的保留效果不如PCA表征。多任务目标部分恢复行为组织结构但降低了检索性能。

Conclusion: 教师-儿童对应关系和行为表型代表两种不同的潜在组织形式，从关联心理测量数据中恢复的潜在结构依赖于所选择的表征学习目标，提示研究者在选择表征学习方法时需要根据下游任务目标进行权衡。
