In a new position-only evaluation that removes temporal leakage through precomputed velocity and acceleration, real-interval WFB reduces ADE by 10.4% relative to the original FFN and remains competitive with parameter-matched ReLU controls, obtaining 2.1% lower mean ADE than the matched FFN with explicit Delta t. Shuffled-interval WFB attains the lowest mean ADE, indicating that the present evidence supports the effectiveness of the wave representation but does not attribute the gain to interval alignment. These results establish WFB as a viable structured feed-forward learning formulation and define a clear basis for subsequent architectural studies.

</details>


### [204] [CoVer: Conflict-Aware Claim Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00508)
*Shuning Zhang,Dai Shi,Bohao Chu,Hui Wang,Yuwei Chuai,Yifan Wang,Jingruo Chen,Simin Li,Xin Yi,Hewu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContraNote大规模真实世界数据集（基于X社区笔记系统），包含33,686条帖子用于证据级冲突解决、54,474个实例用于聚合级优先级评估，并提出CoVer三阶段事实裁决框架（证据模式规范化、事实共识与支持验证），在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体事实核查长期受困于"证据层面冲突"和"聚合层面冲突"两大挑战——错误证据可能伪装成权威新闻来源，导致核查结果被误导。现有方法难以同时应对这两个层面的验证任务，因此需要新的数据集和框架来支持冲突验证。

Method: 构建ContraNote数据集（来自X社区笔记系统），并设计CoVer框架，包含三阶段流水线：1）证据模式规范化（evidence schema normalization）；2）事实共识（factual consensus）；3）支持验证（support verification）。该方法优先处理证据本身而非噪声，防止其对最终裁决造成干扰。同时提出用于评估证据级冲突解决和聚合级优先级的两类任务实例。

Result: 在ContraNote的Conflict任务上CoVer达到86.0%准确率、68.0%宏平均F1、64.5平衡准确率；在Prioritization任务上达到88.5%准确率、88.5宏平均F1、89.2平衡准确率；在CONFACT-HumC上达88.4%准确率，在CONFACT-ModC上达89.4%准确率，均优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: CoVer通过"证据优先"的设计思路有效应对了社交媒体事实核查中证据级与聚合级的双重冲突挑战，在多个数据集上展现了较强的鲁棒性和泛化能力，为冲突验证任务提供了新的数据集资源和解决方案。

Abstract: Social media fact-checking has long been challenged by evidence-level and aggregation-level conflicts, where erroneous evidence mimics authoritative news sources. To capture this challenge and support conflict verification tasks, we present ContraNote, a large-scale real-world dataset curated from X's Community Notes system. It includes 33,686 posts for evaluating evidence-level conflict resolution, and 54,474 instances for evaluating aggregation-level prioritization. Additionally, we propose CoVer, a factual adjudication framework with three-stage pipelines: evidence schema normalization, factual consensus and support verification. This prioritizes evidence over noise to prevent it from compromising the final verdict. Technical evaluations show that CoVer achieves strong performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines across ContraNote (86.0% Acc., 68.0% mac. F1, 64.5 bal. Acc. on Conflict; and 88.5% Acc., 88.5 mac. F1 and 89.2 bal. Acc. on Prioritization), CONFACT-HumC (88.4% Acc.) and CONFACT-ModC (89.4% Acc.).

</details>


### [205] [When the Algorithm Becomes the Brand Crisis: A Sociotechnical Theory of Distributed Responsibility and Accountable Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00510)
*Mohammad Saleh Torkestani,Taha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个社会技术过程理论，用以解释当AI系统、开发者、部署方、供应商和用户之间分散着技术因果、客户控制与治理责任时，利益相关者如何归因责任。该理论区分AI/算法事件、AI相关组织危机与AI相关组织丑闻三个概念，并提出事件构型→参与者特定归因→评估→可能升级为丑闻的级联框架，同时引入"问责透明"作为应对配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益通过聊天机器人、推荐系统、自动化决策和生成式界面参与市场承诺，但传统品牌危机模型无法充分说明在技术因果、客户控制与治理责任分散于多方主体时，利益相关者如何分配责任的问题。

Method: 采用结构化、联邦式整合的综合方法（federated scoping synthesis），对已验证的学术文献和一手来源进行范围性合成，构建概念性社会技术过程理论。

Result: 框架提出：事件构型塑造参与者特定的归因；归因影响能力、诚信、公平和关系评估；公众道德化可能（但未必）将事件升级为丑闻。理论调和了"算法涉入可缓冲负面品牌反应"与"机器人和聊天机器人故障可将责任转移至关联企业"的矛盾发现。证据支持关于责备、信任、满意度、企业评价和传播可信度的有条件的近端推断，强于对持久声誉、品牌权益或市场表现的论断。

Conclusion: 本文提出"问责透明"作为推荐应对配置，综合及时通知、可理解的说明、角色责任承认、补救、纠正证据和追索途径六个要素。理论对条件性近端结果的解释力强于对持久声誉和市场表现的预测。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems increasingly enact market-facing promises through chatbots, recommendation systems, automated decisions, and generative interfaces. Their failures, misuse, and misrepresentation raise a question that conventional brand-crisis models do not fully specify: how do stakeholders assign responsibility when technical causation, customer-facing control, and governance duties are distributed across an AI system, developer, deployer, vendor, and user? This conceptual paper develops a sociotechnical process theory from a structured, federated scoping synthesis of verified academic and primary sources. It distinguishes an AI/algorithmic incident from an AI-related organisational crisis and, in turn, from an AI-related organisational scandal. The framework proposes that incident configuration shapes actor-specific attribution; attribution informs capability, integrity, fairness, and relationship appraisals; and public moralisation may, but need not, escalate an incident into scandal. The theory offers a reconciliation of findings that algorithm involvement can buffer negative brand reactions in some settings while robot and chatbot failures can redirect responsibility to an associated firm in others. It introduces accountable transparency as a proposed response configuration that combines timely notice, an intelligible account, role-responsibility acknowledgement, remedy, evidence of correction, and recourse. The evidence supports conditional, proximal inferences about blame, trust, satisfaction, firm evaluation, and communication credibility more strongly than claims about durable reputation, brand equity, or market performance.

</details>


### [206] [ISO-RAG: Isoperimetric Noise Control for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00513)
*Siyuan Zhang,Hanchen Wang,Dong Wen,Ying Zhang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ISO-RAG是一个几何感知的检索增强生成框架，通过将知识图谱投影到双曲庞加莱球中预计算节点等周轮廓，在检索时剪除虚假边并将搜索空间限制在严格局部的子图中，从而以拓扑纯化方式调控个性化PageRank扩散，解决了图基RAG中的语义漂移和高在线延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密检索无法有效处理多跳问答中的复杂推理路径，而图基RAG虽然能捕获多步关系，但受制于全局图遍历带来的严重语义漂移和高在线延迟。现有方法在准确性和效率之间存在根本性矛盾，亟需一种既能保持图结构推理能力又能消除全局遍历瓶颈的新范式。

Method: ISO-RAG的核心方法包括：(1) 将底层知识图谱嵌入双曲庞加莱球空间以预计算节点级的等周轮廓（isoperimetric profiles）；(2) 利用等周轮廓在检索阶段剪除虚假/噪声边，将搜索空间严格限制在局部子图内；(3) 通过这种拓扑纯化操作调控个性化PageRank（PPR）扩散过程，使其实现精确且低延迟的收敛。

Result: 在多跳QA基准测试上，ISO-RAG相较于最先进基线在检索召回率上平均绝对提升10.0%，在下游精确匹配（EM）指标上平均绝对提升4.3%，实现了更优的精度-效率权衡，并通过根本上消除全局遍历的延迟瓶颈取得了显著效果。

Conclusion: ISO-RAG证明了将双曲几何与图拓扑分析（特别是等周剖分思想）相结合的可行性，为图基RAG提供了一种既能抑制语义漂移又能消除延迟瓶颈的有效方案，展现了良好的准确性与效率兼顾能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates large language models (LLMs) hallucinations, yet conventional dense retrieval struggles with the complex reasoning paths of multi-hop question answering (QA). Graph-based RAG captures multi-step relationships but suffers from severe semantic drift and high online latency due to noisy global graph traversals. Thus, we propose ISO-RAG (ISOperimetric Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a geometry-aware RAG framework. By projecting the underlying knowledge graph into a hyperbolic Poincare ball to precompute node-wise isoperimetric profiles, ISO-RAG prunes spurious edges during retrieval, restricting the search space to a strictly localized subgraph. This topological purification regulates Personalized PageRank (PPR) diffusion driving the retrieval process, ensuring exact and low-latency convergence. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks demonstrate that ISO-RAG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by average absolute gains of 10.0% in retrieval recall and 4.3% in downstream exact match, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off by fundamentally eliminating the latency bottleneck of global traversals. Our source code is available at https://github.com/ZaiizaiZHANG/ISO-RAG.

</details>


### [207] [Feedback-Assisted Trust Propagation over Document Relation Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00543)
*Zhuoheng Li,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustPropRAG，一种通过图结构传播文档可靠性信号的RAG框架。它将文档关系建模为图，结合少量人工反馈锚点，通过多跳传播为整个语料库中的每篇文档估计信任分数，进而改进文档选择和答案生成。实验表明该方法在检索质量和精准匹配上均优于基线，且在稀疏和噪声反馈下保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖的外部语料库可能包含过时、矛盾、嘈杂或不可靠的文档，带来可靠性风险。已有工作利用文档关系改进RAG答案可靠性，但传播范围仅限于被直接比较的文档对，无法将可靠性信号扩展至更广的语料范围。同时，人工反馈成本高昂，需要一种方法用少量反馈锚点覆盖整个语料库。

Method: TrustPropRAG将文档关系建模为图结构，通过多跳传播来估计文档可靠性。具体方法包括：1) 构建文档关系图；2) 将该图与少量人工可靠性反馈相结合，构建一个同时刻画成对文档关系和用户反馈的优化问题；3) 通过求解该优化问题为每篇文档估计信任分数；4) 利用这些信任分数改进可靠文档的选取，并支持信任感知的答案生成。

Result: 评估结果显示TrustPropRAG在检索质量和精确匹配指标上均优于基线方法；在稀疏和带噪声的反馈条件下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TrustPropRAG通过图结构与多跳传播有效放大了有限的反馈可靠性信号，使其覆盖整个语料库，从而在低成本人工标注的前提下提升RAG系统的整体可靠性和下游答案质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems rely on external corpora that may contain outdated, contradictory, noisy, or unreliable documents, introducing reliability risks. Prior work has leveraged document relations to improve the answer reliability of RAG. To propagate reliability signals beyond directly compared document pairs, we propose TrustPropRAG, which structures document relations as a graph and estimates document reliability through multi-hop propagation across the graph. TrustPropRAG anchors this propagation with a limited set of human feedback on document reliability, extending these costly-to-collect feedback-based reliability signals across the whole corpus. Specifically, based on the constructed document relation graph, TrustPropRAG estimates a trust score for each document by formulating and solving an optimization problem that jointly captures pairwise document relations and user feedback. These scores are then used to improve the selection of reliable documents and support trust-aware answer generation. Evaluation results show that TrustPropRAG improves both retrieval quality and exact match over baselines, and remains robust under sparse and noisy feedback.

</details>


### [208] [VoiceLongMemEval: Do Assistants Remember How You Sounded?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00570)
*Ramit Pahwa,Parivesh Priye,Apoorva Beedu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VoiceLongMemEval (VLME)基准，用于评估AI助手在长对话历史中利用副语言元数据（情感标签、韵律描述和语音事件）进行推理的能力。研究发现前沿模型普遍存在"情感差距"（affect gap），当提供文本形式副语言元数据时准确率提升0.09-0.38，音频原生模型可直接从语音中提取线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅将对话历史视为信息检索、时间推理或知识更新任务，忽略了人类-AI交互的基本动态——即"怎么说"这一副语言维度。这一空缺阻碍了对AI助手在现实长对话场景中理解能力的全面评估。

Method: 构建VLME基准，每个问答项的答案依赖于对话轮次中附加的副语言元数据（情感标签、韵律描述符和语音事件），这些信息无法仅从文字恢复。所有条目经过三阶段对抗性门控（adversarial gate），确保强语言模型仅凭转录文本无法回答。评估了前沿闭源和开源权重的模型，并对比音频原生模型。

Result: 评测显示模型普遍存在情感差距：提供文本副语言元数据可使准确率提升0.09至0.38（加入证据提示时可提升至0.61至0.69）；标准ASR流水线系统性地丢弃了这些信号；音频原生模型可直接从语音中提取线索（0.354至0.412对比盲听0.325）。

Conclusion: 现有AI系统在长对话记忆中忽略了副语言信息的价值，导致重要语义丢失。VLME揭示了这一系统性问题，并证明音频原生模型或保留副语言元数据的管线可以部分弥补该差距，为未来多模态对话理解模型的发展提供了评测方向和基准。

Abstract: With the growing scale of multi-agent architectures and large language models, deployed AI assistants are increasingly tasked with reasoning over long, continuous, multi-session conversation histories. Current benchmarks evaluate this dialogue history as information retrieval over long horizon, temporal reasoning, or knowledge updates, while crucially ignoring the fundamental dynamics of human-agent interaction, i.e. how they said it. To address this gap, we present VoiceLongMemEval (VLME) benchmark, where every answer depends on paralinguistic metadata (emotion labels, prosody descriptors, and voice events) attached to conversational turns, which is otherwise unrecoverable from the words alone. Every item passes a three-stage adversarial gate, ensuring that a strong language model fails when given only the transcript. Evaluating leading frontier and open-weight models reveals a pervasive affect gap; providing text-track paralinguistic metadata yields a 0.09 to 0.38 accuracy boost (0.61 to 0.69 when prompted with evidence hints), while standard ASR pipelines systematically discard this signal. Additionally, audio-native models successfully extract these cues directly from speech (0.354 to 0.412 vs. 0.325 blind). Code and dataset will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [209] [Residual Sparsification via Output Importance for Compressing Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00575)
*Seungwoo Jung,Dohyeok Kwon,Seungmin Cha,Junseok Lee,Yeonho Yoo,Chuck Yoo,Gyeongsik Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSER是一种新的残差稀疏化方法，用于MoE架构压缩。它发现现有的独立最小化残差矩阵压缩误差的目标与实际保持模型精度不一致，因此将压缩目标从最小化孤立矩阵误差转向保留专家输出误差，通过输出重要性度量来指导压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽高效但需要大量GPU内存，常需压缩。现有残差稀疏化方法独立压缩每个残差矩阵以最小化其压缩误差，但作者分析发现这一目标与压缩后保持模型精度不一致——因为专家最终输出由多个投影和隐藏表示耦合计算产生，单个矩阵的小误差可能通过传播导致大的输出误差。

Method: 提出PARSER方法，将压缩目标从最小化孤立矩阵误差转变为保持专家输出误差。核心创新是引入"输出重要性"概念，用于衡量每个分量对专家输出误差的实际贡献，从而指导压缩过程。

Result: 在Qwen上精度差距缩小1.41倍，在DeepSeek上缩小1.44倍，同时达到相同的峰值内存减少效果。

Conclusion: PARSER通过将压缩目标对齐到专家输出误差，在相同内存压缩效果下显著提升了压缩后模型的精度保持能力，验证了现有独立残差矩阵压缩目标与模型精度保持之间存在错配，并提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models efficiently, but they demand massive GPU memory. To cope with such demand, models are commonly compressed to reduce their memory footprint. Residual sparsification is a representative compression technique that decomposes each projection matrix of an expert into a shared base matrix and per-expert residual matrix, and then compresses the residuals. Existing sparsification methods compress each residual matrix independently by minimizing its compression error, thereby minimizing the error of each projection matrix. However, our analysis shows that this objective is misaligned with preserving model accuracy after compression. In an expert, the final output is produced through computations coupled across multiple projections and hidden representations. Therefore, even small errors in individual matrices can propagate through hidden representations and projection interactions, leading to large expert output errors and accuracy degradation. To address this misalignment, we propose PARSER, a new residual sparsification method that shifts the compression objective from minimizing isolated matrix errors to preserving the expert output error. PARSER achieves this by introducing output importance, which measures the actual contribution to the expert output error. Our experiments show that, compared with existing methods, PARSER narrows the accuracy gap to the uncompressed model by 1.41$\times$ on Qwen and 1.44$\times$ on DeepSeek, while achieving the same peak memory reduction.

</details>


### [210] [Consistency Without Alignment: Item-Sensitive Language Models Indistinguishable From Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00576)
*Cris Huynh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个从桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》抽象出的强制选择信号任务，证明"项目敏感性"（item-sensitivity）——即模型的选择依赖特定输入而非自身输出先验——虽然是任务胜任力的必要证据，但并非充分证据。在七个语言模型、两个模型家族、去除后训练消融及三种评分规则下，21个模型-规则组合全部可靠地表现出项目敏感性，但其中8个与随机选择无法区分，5个在描述目标上表现差于随机。项目敏感性与随机距离的相关性仅为r=0.30，作者称之为"无对齐的一致性"（consistency without alignment）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估中广泛将项目敏感性、排列一致性或自一致性作为模型任务胜任力的证据，但缺乏独立参考基准来检验这些一致性指标是否真正反映能力，可能导致虚假胜任力判断。

Method: 从桌游《Deception: Murder in Hong Kong》中抽象出一个强制选择信号任务，其中三种参考策略（适应度最大化、后验最大化、均匀随机选择）均可闭式计算。在7个语言模型、2个模型家族、去除后训练消融和3种独立评分规则下，共测试21个模型-规则组合，并通过预注册协议进行统计检验。

Result: 全部21个模型-规则组合都表现出可靠的项目敏感性，但其中8个组合在统计上与忽略输入的随机选择器无法区分，5个组合在描述目标方面表现差于随机。项目敏感性与随机距离的相关性仅为r=0.30。此外，无语用学的字面相似性基线优于大多数模型；在两种基础相似性来源上添加语用层使选择者更接近随机而非贝叶斯参考；标准多选题格式在此几乎不携带任何可测量的内容信号。

Conclusion: 项目敏感性不能单独作为任务胜任力的充分证据；一致性指标若无独立参考量，可能产生"无对齐的一致性"的虚假可靠现象。该结论可推广到任何依赖项目敏感性、排列一致性或自一致性而无独立参考的评估场景。

Abstract: Item-sensitivity, defined as whether a model's choice depends on the specific input rather than on its own output prior, is widely reported as evidence of task competence. We show this evidence is necessary but not sufficient using a forced-choice signalling task abstracted from the board game Deception: Murder in Hong Kong. In this environment, the reference points against which a coordinate should be judged (a fit-maximising strategy, a posterior-maximising strategy, and uniform random selection) are all computable in closed form. Across seven language models, two model families, a post-training ablation, and three independent scoring rules, every one of 21 model-by-rule cells is reliably item-sensitive. Yet 8 of those 21 cells are not statistically distinguishable from a chooser that ignores the item and selects at random, and 5 score worse than random at describing the target. Item-sensitivity and distance from random correlate at only r = 0.30. We call this consistency without alignment and argue it generalises to any evaluation that relies on item-sensitivity, permutation consistency, or self-consistency without an independent reference for the measured quantity. We further find that a literal-similarity baseline with no pragmatics outperforms most tested language models, that adding a pragmatic layer over two baseline similarity sources moves choosers toward random rather than toward the Bayesian reference, and that a standard labelled multiple-choice format carries no measurable content signal here. All results represent the model side of a pre-registered instrument; a matched human condition is designed and piloted but not yet collected.

</details>


### [211] [Same Request, Different Boundary: Evaluating Cybersecurity Assistance across Conversational Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00578)
*Rui Yang,Yang Hong,Yichao Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出3R-Bench基准，包含150个真实网络安全请求及两种对抗性对话场景，评估8个LLM在对话上下文中的安全拒绝机制。研究发现请求历史显著改变模型的拒绝行为（合规率从62.0%升至85.1%），而对话分解则大幅降低合规率（从501/800降至172/800）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在解决复杂问题的同时也可能被滥用于高风险领域。模型提供商需要阻止恶意使用网络安全请求，同时不损害合法防御者的正当协助。然而现有网络安全数据集均未考虑请求的对话上下文，导致对模型安全机制的评估不全面。

Method: 构建3R-Bench（Refusal, Repetition, Revision）基准，包含150个来自真实世界的网络安全请求，并通过两种对抗性对话设置增强：对话历史操纵和对话分解。在400对样本面板中评估8个LLM，同时加入失败反馈（failure feedback）条件以测试恢复能力。

Result: 先前的助手行为会强烈改变对相同请求的响应：在被拒绝历史后合规率为62.0%，在被接受历史后升至85.1%。相反，对话分解下合规率从直接响应时的501/800骤降至172/800，降幅达45.1个百分点。失败反馈仅能恢复这一损失的一小部分。

Conclusion: LLM的安全拒绝行为高度依赖对话上下文，存在通过对话操纵规避安全机制的风险，现有评估方法需将对话状态纳入考量以建立更稳健的安全防线。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can solve complex problems, but their misuse in high-risk domains can lead to severe consequences. Model providers therefore restrict assistance for potentially harmful requests. Refusing all cybersecurity requests would therefore harm legitimate users. Providers need a mechanism to block malicious use without denying legitimate assistance to defenders. Existing cybersecurity-specific datasets evaluate this mechanism, but none considers the conversational context of a request. We introduce 3R-Bench (Refusal, Repetition, and Revision), a benchmark of 150 real-world cybersecurity requests augmented with two adversarial conversational settings, and evaluate eight LLMs on it. Prior assistant behavior strongly changes responses to an unchanged request: among 376 available pairs from a 400-pair panel, compliance rises from 62.0% after refused history to 85.1% after accepted history. The opposite pattern appears under dialogue decomposition. In comparison, compliance falls from 501/800 direct responses to 172/800 after dialogue; among 738 pairs returning model-authored text in both conditions, the decrease is 45.1 points. Failure feedback recovers only a small fraction of this loss.

</details>


### [212] [Socrates went Nuclear: Comparing Interaction Strategies for AI systems in a Learning Context using Brain Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00584)
*Alexandre Clin Deffarges,Nataliya Kosmyna,Pattie Maes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究比较了三种人机交互模式（无限制聊天机器人、苏格拉底式引导机器人、基于脑电信号自适应的非对话式辅导系统）在学习场景中的效果，发现无限制聊天机器人在即时后测中学习增益最高，但脑电数据显示自适应模式下的认知投入更高，暗示无限制AI的成功可能仅是即时测试的表面效果而非深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无限制AI访问在学习中的作用机制：是真正促进深度学习，还是绕过认知努力仅通过直接给出答案使学习者表面得分提高？通过三种交互设计对比，揭示AI辅助学习中的认知投入与学习成效之间的关系。

Method: 50名参与者在核电安全协议（零先验知识领域）上完成学习任务，流程包括教学视频、前测、三种AI驱动的评估阶段（无限制聊天机器人/苏格拉底式受限机器人/基于脑电信号的实时自适应难度调节系统）以及即时后测。使用Muse头带采集所有条件下的脑电信号以计算认知投入指标，并对聊天机器人使用模式进行聚类分析。

Result: 无限制聊天机器人产生的学习增益显著高于两种受限模式（p<.03, d>0.80）；自适应条件下的脑电投入度显著更高（p=.018）。聚类分析显示无限制模式下的多数参与者采用直接获取答案策略，而苏格拉底模式参与者起初尝试推理但逐渐脱离参与。

Conclusion: 无限制AI带来的学习增益并非深层学习的证据，而是即时后测评估的直接产物。聊天机器人直接给出答案绕过认知努力虽然提升了测试成绩，但自适应系统激发更高的认知投入。研究对AI教育应用提出警示：即时表现与认知投入可能不一致。

Abstract: Does unrestricted AI access bypass the cognitive effort required for learning, or does it streamline knowledge acquisition? This paper reports on a study where we compare three designs for user-AI interaction in a learning context: (1) an unrestricted conversational bot like ChatGPT, (2) a pedagogically constrained bot that guides through hints without giving final answers, which we refer to as the Socratic mode; and (3) a non-conversational adaptive tutoring system that adjusts difficulty in real-time based on the user's cognitive engagement derived from the brain signals. Fifty study participants were tasked with learning about nuclear safety protocols, a domain chosen for its zero-prior knowledge baseline. The participants progressed through an instructional video, a pre-test, an AI-driven assessment phase, which varied in the three conditions, and an immediate post-test. The nature of the questions centered primarily on factual knowledge acquisition, but it still required participants to have a global understanding of the concepts in order to answer the questions correctly. A Muse headband was used to derive the cognitive engagement of all users in all conditions. The unrestricted chatbot produced higher learning gains (delta) than both constrained modes (p < .03, d > 0.80), while the adaptive condition generated significantly higher EEG engagement (p = .018). The cluster analysis of chatbot usage and discussion patterns by users showed that most participants in the unrestricted-mode adopted a direct answer-retrieval strategy, while participants in the Socratic-mode initially attempted to reason through the hints before progressively disengaging. Consequently, this also suggests that the success of the unrestricted AI is not an evidence of deeper learning, but rather a result of the immediate post-test evaluation after the training phase.

</details>


### [213] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出控制-数据流分离机制，将多智能体LLM系统中执行关键的协议（如消息路由、输出格式、终止信号）从可优化的提示内容中解耦，用类型化、验证过的程序对象表示控制流，而任务相关语言保留为可优化的数据流，避免提示优化破坏执行协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统中的提示优化面临一个核心问题：同一个提示承担两种纠缠的角色——生成任务相关内容与指定执行关键协议（如消息路由、输出格式、终止信号），代码执行依赖这些协议。用于改进内容生成的提示编辑可能意外损坏协议，导致整个智能体流水线失败。

Method: 观察到执行协议通常是结构化的、而任务相关内容通常是非结构化语言这一关键区别，提出控制-数据流分离机制：执行关键的控制流表示为类型化、经过验证的程序对象，而任务相关语言保持为可优化的数据流用于智能体间通信。这样优化器可以改进多智能体行为，同时不将路由或格式化接口暴露给提示漂移的风险。

Result: 在合成推理、协同审阅生成和保险评级工作流三个场景中，该框架经验证实现了100%的最终协议有效性，同时持续提升任务性能。

Conclusion: 控制-数据流分离是一种有效的架构设计，能够在提示优化过程中同时保证执行协议的健壮性和任务性能的提升，为多智能体LLM系统的提示优化提供了更安全的范式。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [214] [REVISE: Validity-Guided Recovery for Online Revisions in Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00643)
*Ruoling Qi,Xuaner Wu,Penghang Liu,Jian Chen,Yirui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Revise，一个基于有效性引导的运行时系统，用于在结构化Agent工作流中实现细粒度的恢复。通过将修订的增量与数据/控制依赖相交并传播影响，Revise只重算受影响的区域，在保证最新版本正确性的同时最大化保留未受影响的进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent修订在并发执行中暴露了正确性与效率之间的根本权衡：丢弃正在进行的工作能保证最新版本的正确性但浪费了可能仍然有效的进度；复用先前工作保持效率但可能将过期状态传播到输出和工具效果中。现有恢复策略采用粗粒度策略导致这种权衡失衡——要么允许可能过期的状态继续执行，要么从最早冲突处重启或重算线性后缀，从而丢弃未受影响的有效进度。

Method: Revise是有效性引导的细粒度恢复运行时。当修订到达时：(1)将修订的增量与记录的数据依赖和控制依赖求交，并沿部分执行的DAG传播影响以识别受影响的工作；(2)停止无效工作，保留最早冲突点之前已确立有效性的进度，仅重算受影响的区域；(3)对不完整的溯源信息保守地扩展恢复范围，重用的结果在提交前重新验证。

Result: 真实coding-agent追踪分析显示在线恢复机会丰富：118个会话在排队消息投递前保留可观察的工作；167个重叠assistant响应中，入队到完成的重叠在p95达到56.55秒。在300个具挑战性的修订/提交执行中，Revise与最新版本oracle匹配，无过期输出或效果。在未修改的LangGraph和LLMCompiler应用中（使用Qwen3-14B），相比全量重启减少40.6-56.0%的模型调用，相比后缀重算减少31.3-43.6%。在服务压力下，修订到正确完成token减少了13.26%，SLO有效吞吐提升3.07-5.43%。

Conclusion: Revise通过在结构化Agent工作流中实现细粒度的、有效性引导的恢复，成功调和了正确性与效率的权衡，既能保证最新版本语义正确（无过期状态或效果），又能最大化保留未受影响的有效计算进度，显著降低模型调用开销并改善服务SLO。

Abstract: Agent revisions expose a fundamental correctness--efficiency trade-off during concurrent execution. Discarding ongoing work preserves latest-version correctness but wastes progress that may remain valid, whereas reusing prior work preserves efficiency but risks propagating stale state into outputs and tool effects. Existing recovery strategies resolve this trade-off in an imbalanced way with coarse-grained policies: they either favor efficiency by allowing potentially stale work to continue, or favor correctness by restarting the workflow or recomputing a linear suffix from the earliest conflict, thereby discarding unaffected progress. We present \textsc{Revise}, a validity-guided runtime for fine-grained recovery in structured agent workflows. When a revision arrives, \textsc{Revise} first intersects its delta with recorded data and control dependencies and propagates the resulting impact through the partially executed DAG to identify affected work. It then stops invalid work, preserves validity-established progress beyond the earliest conflict, and recomputes only the affected region. Incomplete provenance conservatively expands recovery, while reused results are revalidated before commit. Analysis of real coding-agent traces show online recovery opportunities: 118 sessions retain observable work before a queued later message is delivered; across 167 overlapping assistant responses, enqueue-to-completion overlap reaches 56.55~s at p95. Across 300 challenging revision/commit executions, \textsc{Revise} matches a latest-version oracle with no stale outputs or effects. On unmodified LangGraph and LLMCompiler applications using Qwen3-14B, it reduces model calls by 40.6--56.0\% relative to full restart and by 31.3--43.6\% relative to suffix recomputation. Under serving pressure, it further reduces revision-to-correct-completion tokens by 13.26\% and improves SLO goodput by 3.07--5.43\%.

</details>


### [215] [DramaChain Bench: An End-to-End Benchmark for Short-Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00646)
*Haoyuan Shi,Mingtao Chen,Shuo Jiang,Ziyan Chen,Xuyi Sheng,Yiming Liu,Ying Zhang,Miao Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Fanyang Lu,Songyuanyi Lu,Xiele Wu,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Richeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DramaChain Bench，这是首个覆盖短剧全制作链路（剧本→分镜→关键帧→镜头视频→成片）各阶段评估的基准。它基于统一的维度系统（五个评估轴、63个叶子维度），由商业短剧平台校准的智能体和专业标注系统构成，含5,785个标注项、17,488个有效评分及255,925条可追溯归因记录，验证了上游缺陷沿流水线级联传播。其Agentic Judge以0.918的PLCC复现模型排名，实现零标注成本的模型准入评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估视频生成阶段，使用预先撰写的输入而非真实上游流水线产物，无法回答两个关键问题：各阶段是否遵循原始剧本意图（而非仅即时输入提示），以及多镜头组装成多集成片后是否保持连贯性。

Method: 提出DramaChain Bench，包含三大系统：(1) DramaChain Dimensions——统一维度系统，五个评估轴在各阶段实例化，解析为63个叶子维度；(2) DramaChain Agent——依据商业短剧平台在流程和成片质量上校准，支持跨模型逐阶段公平比较；(3) DramaChain Labeling System——5,785个项目均由三位专业标注员独立评分，缺陷进行时空定位并从预定义缺陷列表中选择，产生17,488个有效分数和255,925条可追溯归因记录。此外，DramaChain Agentic Judge进行多轮智能体证据收集后按逐项检查清单评分。

Result: 人工标注确认上游缺陷沿流水线级联传播，证明成片质量并非仅由视频生成阶段决定。DramaChain Agentic Judge在全部叶子维度上自动评分，以0.918的平均PLCC复现模型排名，足以实现零标注成本的新模型准入。

Conclusion: DramaChain Bench验证了全链路阶段一致性评估的必要性，揭示了短剧质量受上游环节缺陷级联影响的规律，并提供了一种接近人类标注质量的自动化评估方案，可为短剧全链路模型开发提供可靠且低成本的评估工具。

Abstract: Commercial short-drama production follows a multi-stage chain: script, storyboard, keyframe imagery, shot-level video, and the finished short drama. Most existing benchmarks evaluate solely the video-generation stage using pre-authored inputs instead of real upstream pipeline outputs. This leaves two critical questions unanswerable: whether each stage adheres to the original script intent (rather than only its immediate input prompt), and whether disparate shots remain coherent after assembly into multi-episode releases. We present DramaChain Bench, the first short-drama benchmark that evaluates every stage of the complete production chain. It is built upon three in-house systems sharing one dimension system, DramaChain Dimensions: five evaluation axes instantiated at every stage, resolving into 63 leaf dimensions. DramaChain Agent is calibrated against commercial short-drama platforms in both workflow and finished short-drama quality, enabling stage-wise fair comparison across models. DramaChain Labeling System has each of the 5,785 items scored independently by three professional annotators, with all defects spatio-temporally localised and selected from a predefined defect list. This process produces 17,488 valid scores and 255,925 traceable attribution records. The human annotations confirm that upstream defects cascade across the pipeline, demonstrating that final episode quality is not governed by video generation alone. DramaChain Agentic Judge then scores every leaf dimension automatically, gathering evidence over multiple agentic rounds before judging against a per-item checklist; it reproduces the model ranking at a mean PLCC of 0.918, enough to admit new models at no annotation cost.

</details>


### [216] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种环境锚定审计方法，在进化式Contexto搜索任务中对语言模型智能体的自我报告进行验证。研究发现智能体的陈述置信度、继承推理和基于适应度的报告选择三个关键假设均不成立，表明智能体自我报告应被视为需要环境验证的主张，而非其可靠性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多地提出动作、观察外部反馈并解释自身行为，其置信度和推理过程被方便地用作监控信号。但作者指出"便利不等于验证"——这些自我报告缺乏对环境真实结果的锚定，因此需要系统性审计来检验自我报告的可信度。

Method: 作者引入环境锚定审计方法：让LM智能体操作进化式Contexto搜索，其中每个有效猜测都通过反馈函数获得精确排名（无需人工标注）。共进行200次运行，覆盖5种配置和3个模型家族，产生12,249份自我报告。测试三个假设：(1)陈述置信度已校准；(2)继承的推理影响后续提议；(3)基于适应度的选择提高报告质量。通过对照干预（754份继承推理）量化真实推理的收益上限。

Result: 三个假设全部失败。操作者将前100名成功率夸大了4.8到9.3倍；校准与区分能力在不同模型家族间出现分离。对754份继承推理的受控干预表明，真实推理的测得收益上限约为250个排名。基于适应度的选择和随机选择均未产生可检测的选择差异或父代到子代的报告准确性传递，尽管两种搜索行为存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 智能体自我报告不应被当作自身可靠性的证据，应视为需要对照环境进行验证的主张。该研究强调了在AI监控中环境锚定验证的重要性，提示依赖智能体自信度和解释作为监督信号的实践需要重新审视。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Task-Specific Prompt with Global Context for Multi-Task Graph Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00047)
*Zhiyang Qiu,Yangtao Wang,Xiaocui Li,Yanzhao Xie,Siyuan Chen,Wensheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TPGC框架，通过任务先验和结构先验双重初始化机制来提升多任务图预训练模型在下游任务中的提示（prompt）质量，在6个基准上实现少样本场景下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习中的多任务图预训练框架使用随机初始化的提示，导致提示空间与预训练目标及图结构特征之间对齐不良，削弱了提示表示的任务相关性、结构感知能力和可迁移性。

Method: 提出TPGC双先验提示初始化方案。包含两个模块：(1) Task-Prior Injection Module，在辅助图上进行短的同源多任务预训练，使提示初始化继承多个前置任务相关的优化偏好；(2) Structure-Prior Injection Module，基于任务感知表示提取辅助图中可迁移的全局结构上下文，通过聚合含结构信息的节点嵌入转化为逐层提示向量。

Result: 在涵盖节点分类和图分类的6个主流基准上，TPGC在少样本设置下一致地优于现有最先进基线，且下游可调参数更少、运行时间更低。

Conclusion: TPGC通过显式建模任务先验与结构先验的协同作用，有效解决了随机提示初始化导致的表示对齐问题，显著提升了图提示学习在下游任务中的性能与效率。

Abstract: Graph prompt learning is an effective paradigm to adapt pre-trained graph models to downstream tasks in low-resource scenarios. However, existing multi-task graph pre-training frameworks generally use randomly initialized prompts, leading to poor alignment between the prompt space, pretext objectives and graph structural characteristics. This greatly weakens the task relevance, structural awareness and transferability of prompt representations. To address this challenge, we propose TPGC, a dual-prior prompt initialization solution that explicitly models the synergy between task prior and structural prior. Specifically, the Task-Prior Injection Module first conducts a short homologous multi-task pre-training on an auxiliary graph, enabling prompt initialization to inherit optimization preferences associated with multiple pretext tasks. Built on the task-aware representations, the Structure-Prior Injection Module further extracts transferable global structural context from the auxiliary graph, converting it into layer-wise prompt vectors by aggregating structurally informative node embeddings. Extensive experiments on 6 mainstream benchmarks covering node and graph classification show that TPGC achieves consistently better performance under few-shot settings than state-of-the-art baselines, with fewer downstream tunable parameters and lower runtime. The code is available at https://github.com/Virgilqiu/TPGC

</details>


### [218] [REAL-Q: E2E LLM Quantization via Dynamic Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00049)
*Qian Zhang,Yaoming Li,Zhewen Tan,Yanshu Wang,Heng Lu,Kun Su,Zongwei Lv,Wenhan Yu,Yongge Ma,Yinjun Han,Ruikuang Liu,Tong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REAL-Q提出了一种新的后训练量化范式，通过端到端对齐的全局损失代理和细粒度动态逐块梯度下降，在每个128列块后实时刷新Hessian，配合滑动窗口机制缓解跨层误差传播，在LLaMA-3.1和Qwen3系列模型的W4A16量化中显著降低了端到端KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的PTQ方法存在"信息错位"问题：一方面为保持解析可解性而严重近似全局损失（丢弃跨通道耦合、进行输出行分组），另一方面在整层范围内冻结Hessian，无法随损失景观按列变化而刷新。这种在可解析性和准确性之间的妥协限制了量化性能。

Method: REAL-Q针对全局损失的端到端对齐代理进行优化，而非为了解析可解性稀释目标。核心采用细粒度的动态逐块梯度下降，在每128列块后实时应用；同时引入滑动窗口机制实现平滑的跨层过渡，有效缓解误差在网络中的传播。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1（8B和70B）以及Qwen3（0.6B-32B）的W4A16量化设置下，与最先进的全局引导方法相比，REAL-Q将端到端KL散度最多降低了约49%。

Conclusion: REAL-Q通过打破"全局精度与解析可解性"的固有取舍，以实时的端到端对齐优化方式克服了信息错位问题，为在高资源约束下部署LLM提供了更优的量化方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying large language models (LLMs) under strict resource constraints. State-of-the-art PTQ methods quantize each layer with a single closed-form second-order solver: to remain analytically tractable, they heavily approximate the global loss (dropping cross-channel coupling, pooling output rows into groups), and they then freeze the resulting Hessian across the entire layer, with no way to refresh it as the loss landscape shifts column by column--a phenomenon we call information misalignment. We propose REAL-Q (Real-time E2E-loss Aligned LLM Quantization), a novel PTQ paradigm that breaks this compromise: instead of diluting the objective for the sake of analytic tractability, REAL-Q targets an end-to-end-aligned surrogate of the global loss and refines it via fine-grained, dynamic Block-wise Gradient Descent applied after every column block (128 columns). By coupling this fine-grained correction with a sliding window mechanism for smooth cross-layer transitions, REAL-Q effectively mitigates error propagation across the network. On LLaMA-3.1 (8B and 70B) and Qwen3 (0.6B-32B) at W4A16, REAL-Q reduces end-to-end KL divergence by up to ~49% relative to state-of-the-art globally-guided methods.

</details>


### [219] [Convergence issues in Relational Concept Analysis based on AOC-posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00054)
*Xavier Dolques,Agnès Braud,Alain Gutierrez,Marianne Huchard,Florence Le Ber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Relational Concept Analysis (RCA)在AOC-poset结构下的收敛性问题。RCA是一种多关系数据的FCA扩展，本文揭示了当使用AOC-poset替代概念格时，RCA迭代过程可能无法收敛，分析了无法收敛的原因和可确保收敛的条件，并提出了一种保留AOC-poset结构的收敛变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCA依赖迭代过程，在基于概念格的原始设置中可保证收敛，但AOC-poset因其更小、更易计算而被广泛使用，然而在AOC-poset上实例化RCA会丧失收敛性保证。深入研究这一收敛性丧失问题对于扩展RCA在实际应用中的可用性至关重要。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了RCA在AOC-poset设置下收敛性丧失的原因，识别了可以确保收敛的具体条件，探讨了如何通过数据变换恢复收敛性，并提出了一种不删除已创建关系属性的收敛变体（保证收敛但可能产生指向最终结构中不存在概念的属性）。

Result: 证明了在一般情况下AOC-poset上的RCA不再保证收敛，给出了可确保收敛的条件，提出了数据变换以恢复收敛性的方案，并提出了一种保证收敛的变体方法。

Conclusion: AOC-poset上的RCA收敛性问题是可以被刻画和解决的：通过特定条件约束或数据变换可恢复收敛，而一个简单的关系属性永不删除的变体虽以属性冗余为代价，但能稳健地保证收敛。

Abstract: Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) is an approach for conceptual classification building and rule discovery from a binary table describing a set of objects by a set of attributes. Extensions have been proposed to deal with non-binary and more complex data, such as Relational Concept Analysis (RCA) for multi-relational data. RCA aims to highlight groups of objects characterized by their relationships with other groups of objects. The richer and more complex nature of the underlying data allows RCA to produce richer results than FCA, at the expense of higher computational and interpretive complexity. The most commonly used conceptual classification structure in FCA is the concept lattice. However, in many applications, concept lattice substructures, such as AOC-posets, are preferred over the full lattice, either to mitigate combinatorial blow-up or to focus on the most informative parts of the structure. Indeed, in an AOC-poset, only concepts introducing an object or an attribute are represented, which makes AOC-posets smaller and easier to compute and use than concept lattices. Although RCA was originally defined on concept lattices, it can also be instantiated on AOC-posets. RCA is iterative and its convergence is guaranteed in the lattice-based setting, but this guarantee is lost when using AOC-posets. In this paper, we investigate this loss of convergence in detail. We show why convergence is no longer guaranteed in the general case, identify conditions under which it can still be ensured, and discuss how a dataset can be transformed to recover convergence. We also propose a convergent variant of the process, which preserves the AOC-poset structure: relational attributes, once created, are never removed, which guarantees convergence at the price of attributes that may refer to concepts absent from the final structures.

</details>


### [220] [DISTAL: Distillation and Self-Supervised Pretraining for Structure-Agnostic Materials Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00059)
*Weiran Wang,Xintong Huo,Yueying Wang,Yusi Fan,Wenyan Wang,Xin Feng,Ruihao Xin,Lan Huang,Kewei Li,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DISTAL框架，一种面向结构无关的材料属性预测的双重先验方法，结合自监督组分预训练与结构感知知识蒸馏，在39个基准任务中37个任务超过参考基准，实现了仅基于组分信息的鲁棒预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料属性预测在小数据场景下仍具挑战性，许多目标属性仅有有限的标注样本。目前预测精度最高的模型通常依赖晶体结构信息，这限制了其在早期筛选阶段（此时结构信息稀缺或不可用）的应用。因此需要一种不依赖结构输入的高精度预测方法。

Method: DISTAL采用双先验框架：(1) 使用145个组分派生描述符从大规模虚拟组分空间中进行自监督组分预训练，学习可迁移的组分表征；(2) 将预训练ALIGNN教师模型中的结构知识蒸馏到组分条件学生模型中，使训练时可用结构先验而推理时无需结构输入。最终将显式组分描述符、预训练潜特征与蒸馏结构特征三类信号集成到统一的预测管线中。

Result: 在39个基准任务上，融合三类信号的多模态配置在37个任务上优于参考基准，在所有评估的特征组合中取得最强整体性能，表明组分预训练与结构蒸馏提供互补先验。

Conclusion: 组分预训练与结构知识蒸馏是互补的先验来源，为小数据材料信息学中仅基于组成的预测提供了实用且稳健的路径。

Abstract: Materials property prediction remains difficult in low-data settings, where many target properties are supported by only a limited number of labeled samples. Models with the strongest predictive accuracy often depend on crystal structures, which restricts their use in early-stage screening when structural information is limited or unavailable. To address this challenge, we propose DISTAL, a dual-prior framework for structure-agnostic materials property prediction that combines self-supervised compositional pretraining with structure-aware knowledge distillation. DISTAL first learns transferable compositional representations from a large virtual composition space using 145 composition-derived descriptors. It then distills structural knowledge from a pretrained ALIGNN teacher into a composition-conditioned student. This setting allows structural priors to be used during training without requiring structural inputs at inference. By integrating explicit compositional descriptors, pretrained latent features, and distilled structural features within a unified prediction pipeline, DISTAL captures complementary signals that are difficult to recover from any single representation alone. Across 39 benchmark tasks, the best-performing multimodal configuration combines all three signals, and improves over the reference benchmark on 37 tasks. DISTAL achieves the strongest overall performance among all evaluated feature combinations. These results indicate that compositional pretraining and structural distillation provide complementary priors and offer a practical route to robust composition-only prediction in small-data materials informatics. The source code and the pre-trained models are anonymously available at: https://osf.io/eq96d/overview?view_only=451617f42f7849e08750bd1852b48980 and will be released at the official link after acceptance.

</details>


### [221] [ReNFT: Repairing Mode Collapse in Reward Post-Training via Internal Probability-Mass Recalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00061)
*Yuchen Bao,Chao Wen,Haowei Wang,Ruoxin Chen,Donghao Luo,Jiahui Zhan,Wenjian Huang,Shen Chen,Yiting Wang,Taiping Yao,Chengjie Wang,Shouhong Ding,Jianguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReNFT方法，通过内部概率质量重校准来修复已发生模式坍塌但保持高奖励的扩散模型适配器，无需外部信号或接口干预，在保留奖励的同时显著恢复多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励后训练的扩散生成器不可避免地会将概率质量集中于少数奖励偏好的模式上，导致模式坍塌并消除提示内的多样性。现有缓解方法依赖外部信号或接口（如感知目标、参考正则化调整、文本编码器修改），但都无法修复已经坍塌的适配器，同时保留已获得的奖励。

Method: ReNFT通过内部概率质量重校准来修复高奖励、低多样性的适配器。具体包括三个关键步骤：(1) 无条件探针优先选择"anti-hub"提示，即最容易暴露提示无关偏置的提示；(2) 两条策略主导的混合路线从相同提示和初始噪声生成匹配的反事实提案——一条探查冻结基础方向的被抑制替代方案，另一条暴露后训练的无条件倾向；(3) 基于奖励排序和自适应翻转守卫分配拉/推角色，并通过联合配对的无条件频率变换（NFT）更新实现修复。

Result: 在PickScore和GenEval基准上，ReNFT分别保留了NFT奖励的98.9%和99.0%，同时将DreamSim多样性指标分别提升了58.8%和55.0%。

Conclusion: ReNFT证明了坍塌是抑制而非删除——在线后训练主要重新分配预训练继承的能力而非学习新视觉内容，因此可以从生成器内部逆转模式坍塌，为外部干预提供了互补的替代方案。

Abstract: Reward post-training of diffusion generators inevitably concentrates probability mass on a few reward-favored modes, a mode collapse that erases within-prompt diversity. Existing methods for mitigating collapse rely on external signals or interfaces, augmenting the reward with perceptual objectives, adjusting reference regularization, or modifying the text encoder, but none repairs an adapter that has already collapsed while preserving the acquired reward. We observe that online post-training primarily reallocates probability mass over capabilities inherited from pretraining rather than learning new visual content. Collapse is therefore suppression, not deletion, and can be reversed from within the generator. We propose ReNFT, which repairs a high-reward, low-diversity adapter through internal probability-mass recalibration. Unconditional probes first prioritize "anti-hub" prompts where the prompt-independent bias is easiest to expose. Two policy-dominated mixed routes then generate matched counterfactual proposals from the same prompt and initial noise, one probing the frozen base direction for suppressed alternatives and the other exposing the post-trained unconditional tendency. Reward ranking with an adaptive flipping guard assigns pull and push roles, and a joint-and-paired NFT update realizes the repair. On PickScore and GenEval, ReNFT retains 98.9% and 99.0% of NFT's reward while improving DreamSim-Div by 58.8% and 55.0%, respectively, offering a complementary alternative to external interventions.

</details>


### [222] [Attention Sensitivity Is Not Enough: Dissociating Attention-Level and Behavioural In-Context Learning under Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00064)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Peng He,He Hu,Yin Yuan,ShengShuo Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑注意力层级的in-context learning (ICL)代理指标能否在作为训练目标被优化后仍然可靠。作者形式化了In-Context Sensitivity (ICS)指标，并在Llama-2-7B上进行四臂消融实验，发现最大化ICS的正则化器可将其驱动到接近几何上限，但行为层面的ICL-GAP几乎为零且MMLU准确率大幅下降，揭示了Goodhart解离现象。注意力变得尖锐且跨前缀近乎不相交，但关注的却是格式化和演示token而非标签。研究呼吁注意力级ICL代理需经行为差距验证后才能作为训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断方法常用注意力变化来衡量模型的上下文敏感性，并以此作为ICL保持的代理指标。然而这些代理指标在被当作训练目标优化后是否仍然可靠未经系统验证。本文的核心动机是探究这种"代理指标被优化后失效"的Goodhart效应是否存在于注意力层级的ICL诊断中。

Method: 论文形式化两个指标：ICS（ICL敏感性，度量匹配与不匹配演示前缀下最后token注意力的平均行距离）和ICL-GAP（相同前缀间行为准确率差距）。在Llama-2-7B上设计四臂消融实验，包括ICS最大化正则化器、行为门控、锚定预训练计算的目标等设置，结合端点统计和随机标签协议分析机制。

Result: ICS最大化正则化器将ICS驱动至1.413（接近其几何上限的0.5%以内），但ICL-GAP保持接近零，MMLU准确率从0.371降至0.279，呈现明显的Goodhart解离。端点统计显示注意力变得尖锐且在跨前缀间近乎不相交，但路由到格式化和演示体token而非标签。随机标签协议确认行为探针家族在相同检查点保持动态范围。行为门控可部分缓解效应，锚定预训练计算的目标能维持高MMLU、中等ICS区域。

Conclusion: 主要教训是诊断性的：注意力层级的ICL代理指标只有在对行为差距进行验证之后，才配得上被当作训练目标使用。为了防止微调侵蚀ICL能力，不应单纯依赖注意力变化作为代理，而需以行为层面的验证作为最终基准。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets large language models adapt to new tasks from demonstrations, and fine-tuning can erode this behaviour. Many preservation diagnostics inspect attention: if attention changes when demonstrations change, the model is treated as context-sensitive. This paper asks how far that proxy can be trusted once it is optimised. We formalise \emph{In-Context Sensitivity} (ICS), the average row distance between last-token attention on matched and mismatched demonstration prefixes, and pair it with \emph{ICL-GAP}, the behavioural accuracy gap between the same prefixes. In a controlled four-arm ablation on Llama-2-7B, an ICS-maximising regulariser ($\armKL$) drives ICS to $1.413$, within $0.5\%$ of its geometric ceiling. The behavioural readout tells a different story: ICL-GAP stays near zero and MMLU accuracy moves from $0.371$ to $0.279$, a Goodhart dissociation of the bounded attention proxy. Endpoint statistics locate the mechanism: attention grows sharp and near-disjoint across prefixes yet routes to formatting and demonstration-body tokens rather than labels. A random-label protocol confirms that the behavioural probe family retains dynamic range at the same checkpoints. In a constructive sweep, behaviour gating partially mitigates the effect, while objectives anchored to pretrained computation hold the high-MMLU, moderate-ICS region that divergence maximisers leave. The main lesson is diagnostic: attention-level ICL proxies earn their place as training targets only after validation against behavioural gaps.

</details>


### [223] [RW-LoRA: Communication-Efficient Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning via Random Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00078)
*Xingran Chen,Rohit Bhagat,Ghadir Ayache,Rawad Bitar,Yanmin Gong,Salim El Rouayheb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机游走的去中心化LoRA微调方法，单个模型token在网络中顺序遍历并在本地更新，无需全局同步，大幅降低通信与计算开销并避免聚合误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式LoRA方法（集中式聚合和基于gossip的去中心化）均存在显著通信开销以及多模型更新同时聚合引入的误差问题。

Method: 提出随机游走式LoRA微调方案：维护单个模型token在网络中顺序遍历各节点，利用本地微调目标进行顺序更新，消除全局同步需求。

Result: 在多个NLP任务和图拓扑上，该方法在保持竞争性任务性能的同时，相比gossip-based LoRA显著降低了通信和计算开销，并在标准假设下为非凸目标提供了严格的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 随机游走式LoRA为分布式微调提供了一种高效、低通信且避免聚合误差的新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA have become a standard approach for adapting large foundation models. Adopting fine-tuning to distributed settings faces several challenges. Most existing distributed LoRA methods rely on centralized aggregation, and gossip-based decentralized LoRA requires repeated synchronization among multiple model copies. Both methods incur significant communication overhead and introduce errors due to simultaneous aggregation of multiple model updates. In this paper, we take a different perspective and propose a random-walk-based LoRA fine-tuning scheme. Instead of maintaining multiple model replicas, a single model token traverses the network and is updated sequentially using local fine-tuning objectives. This design eliminates the need for global synchronization, substantially reduces communication and computation costs, and avoids aggregation errors. We provide rigorous convergence guarantees for non-convex objectives under standard assumptions. Through empirical results on multiple NLP tasks and graph topologies, we show that the proposed method achieves competitive task performance with substantially less communication and computation than gossip-based LoRA.

</details>


### [224] [Stochastic complexity of vectors containing cluster structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00084)
*Daniel Nicorici,Olli Yli-Harja,Jaakko Astola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于归一化最大似然（NML）模型计算包含聚类结构的编码向量的随机概率（最短编码长度）问题。作者提出了一种递归公式用于高效计算NML模型的归一化常数，将时间复杂度从多项式降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于最小描述长度（MDL）原则的数据聚类中，需要计算包含聚类结构的向量的最短编码长度以估计最佳聚类数和聚类结构。然而基于NML模型的直接计算需要与向量规模和聚类数相关的多项式时间，效率低下，需要更高效的算法。

Method: 提出一种递归公式（recursion formula），用于高效计算NML模型的归一化常数（normalizing constant），从而将计算随机概率的时间复杂度从原来的多项式时间降低为线性时间（相对于向量规模和聚类数）。

Result: 证明该问题在计算上是可处理的（tractable），新的递归公式使时间复杂度相对于向量规模和聚类数为线性，大幅优于此前多项式时间的计算方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入递归公式，成功将NML模型下包含聚类结构的随机概率计算从多项式复杂度优化为线性复杂度，为基于MDL原则的聚类分析（如最佳聚类数估计和聚类结构选择）提供了高效的计算工具。

Abstract: This paper studies the problem of computing the stochastic probability (shortest code length) of the encoded vectors containing cluster structure using Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) model. This is of great theoretical and practical importance in data clustering based on Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle, such as for estimating the best number of clusters and best cluster structure for the data. Straightforward computation of the shortest code length of the vector containing cluster structure based on the NML model requires polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters. We show that this is a tractable problem by introducing a recursion formula for the efficient computation of normalizing constant from the NML model. The time complexity of the new formula is linear opposed to previous polynomial time with respect to the size of the vector and number of clusters.

</details>


### [225] [Foundation models for electricity price forecasting and battery arbitrage: Can they replace market-specific forecasting models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00089)
*Arkadiusz Lipiecki,Rafał Weron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了五个基础模型家族的九个变体（零样本模式）与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型，在德国、波兰和西班牙市场2021-2025年间进行评估。结果显示只有TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上持续显著优于基准，但其统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势——TabPFN在无限出价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而Distributional Deep Neural Network基准在低风险容忍度下更有利可图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型承诺以极少或无需任务特定训练即可实现准确预测，但它们能否替代专门为电价预测设计的模型仍不明确。作者旨在回答这一问题，并通过点预测、概率预测精度及经济价值（电池储能套利）多维度评估来全面验证。

Method: 选取九个基础模型变体（来自五个基础模型家族），在零样本模式下与两个最先进的电价预测基准模型进行比较。评估涵盖德国、波兰和西班牙三个电力市场2021-2025年的数据，从点预测精度、概率预测精度以及电池储能套利中的经济价值三个维度进行综合评估。

Result: TabPFN模型在所有三个市场和所有统计指标上持续且显著优于基准模型；但TabPFN仅在无限出价和风险较高的分位数策略下表现最佳，而Distributional Deep Neural Network基准模型在低风险容忍度情况下更有利可图。统计优势并未直接转化为经济优势。

Conclusion: 基础模型无法普遍替代特定市场的模型，其价值既取决于模型架构，也取决于具体的决策问题。统计预测精度与经济价值之间的脱节表明，评估预测模型时需要结合下游应用场景进行多维度考量。

Abstract: Foundation models promise accurate forecasts with little or no task-specific training, but whether they can replace models designed specifically for electricity price forecasting remains unclear. We compare nine variants from five foundation model families, evaluated in zero-shot mode, with two state-of-the-art electricity price forecasting benchmarks in Germany, Poland, and Spain over 2021-2025. Their performance is assessed in terms of point and probabilistic forecasting accuracy, as well as economic value in battery energy storage arbitrage. Only the TabPFN models consistently and significantly outperform the benchmarks across all three markets and all statistical measures. However, this statistical dominance does not translate directly into economic dominance: TabPFN performs best under unlimited bids and riskier quantile-based strategies, whereas the Distributional Deep Neural Network benchmark is more profitable when risk tolerance is lower. Thus, foundation models cannot universally replace market-specific models, and their value depends on both model architecture and the decision problem.

</details>


### [226] [Assessing Alignment and Stability of Feature Importance Explanations via Weight of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00090)
*Eddie Conti,Claudio Daka,Álvaro Parafita,Antonio L. Alfeo,Axel Brando,Mario G. C. A. Cimino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将特征重要性方法（FIMs）嵌入基于证据权重（Weight of Evidence, WoE）的假设检验框架中，用以量化观测证据对特定特征重要性假说的支持程度，从而实现对FIMs的可解释性和可靠性的对比式评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征重要性方法（FIMs）虽广泛用于可解释AI，但单独的归因分数往往无法充分揭示模型背后的推理过程，缺乏一种原理性的框架来评估这些归属结果与先验知识、真实标签等参考假说之间的一致性及其变异性。

Method: 将FIM嵌入WoE假设检验框架中，量化证据对特征重要性假说的支持强度；参考假说可来自领域知识、真实标签或FIM自身；提供了将WoE与归因方差联系起来的理论结果。

Result: 在LIME和SHAP解释上，针对不同参考假说设定的实验验证了该框架的适用性与灵活性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种通过对比性、基于证据的视角来评估FIMs的补充工具，能够同时捕获FIMs与先验知识的一致性和自身的变异性。

Abstract: Feature importance Methods (FIMs) are widely used in Explainable AI to interpret model predictions, yet attribution scores alone often provide limited insight into the underlying reasoning process. In this work, we introduce a novel perspective by embedding FIMs within a hypothesis-testing framework based on Weight of Evidence (WoE). We quantify how strongly the observed evidence supports any given hypothesis on feature importance. The reference hypothesis can stem from domain knowledge, ground truth, or be derived from the FIM itself. This formulation enables a principled evaluation of FIMs, capturing both their alignment with prior knowledge and their variability. We further provide theoretical results linking WoE to attribution variance. Empirical results shows the applicability and flexibility of our strategy analyzing LIME and SHAP explanations in settings with different reference hypotheses. Overall, our framework offers a complementary tool for assessing FIMs through a contrastive, evidence-based lens.

</details>


### [227] [Safin-1: Safety from Within through Memory-Native State Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00092)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Zhekai Chen,Cheng Jin,Jingnan Zheng,Yi Zhang,Zhongtian Ma,Jiawei Zhou,Sirui Chen,Qiaosheng Zhang,Xiang Wang,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Youbang Sun,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"内在安全"(Safety from Within)原则，并推出Safin-1基础模型家族，通过记忆锚定路由(MARCH)架构在模型原生计算中实现安全能力的状态化表示与演化，将安全从外部约束转变为模型自身的可维护状态能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程复杂任务要求基础模型在长时间交互中累积信息、维护内部状态并持续适应。现有安全机制依赖于外部防护或事后对齐(如监督微调)，但这将安全视为一种外部施加的行为约束，而非模型的内在属性。作者认为安全应是模型自身的固有特性，因此需要探索从模型内部原生计算出发的安全实现路径。

Method: Safin-1基于记忆-锚定跨上下文历史路由(MARCH)网络架构构建。MARCH维护结构化记忆状态，通过内容条件路由选择性检索相关历史信息。该架构支持对持久能力状态进行测试时适应，无需重复修改主干网络，可在共享基座上实现受控特化。具体通过"安全状态"(Safety State)在下游安全任务上实现状态化适应。

Result: 在安全任务上通过安全状态展现了有效的状态化适应能力，获得显著安全提升。在通用能力、长上下文理解、检索和效率等多个维度的评估中均验证了Safin-1的有效性。

Conclusion: 路由状态接口将情境记忆与持久能力适应统一到模型原生计算中，将记忆从被动的历史记录重新定义为维持和演化模型行为的主动基质。这为"安全作为状态原生且可自适应维护的能力"提供了可行路径。作者指出这仅是"内在安全"的初步架构探索，还有大量工作需要推进以全面实现该愿景。

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require foundation models to accumulate information, maintain internal states, and adapt over extended interactions. Safety should be an intrinsic property of the model itself, rather than a behavioral constraint relying solely on external safeguards or post-hoc alignment such as supervised fine-tuning. This motivates Safety from Within, where safety-relevant capabilities are represented and invoked through the model's native computation. We present Safin-1, a family of foundation models realizing this principle through memory routing and state evolution. Safin-1 is built on Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that maintains structured memory states and selectively retrieves relevant historical information through content-conditioned routing. It supports test-time adaptation of persistent capability states without repeatedly modifying the backbone, enabling controlled specialization over a shared foundation. We investigate this interface on downstream safety tasks through a Safety State, demonstrating effective state-based adaptation with substantial safety improvements. More broadly, the routed-state interface unifies contextual memory and persistent capability adaptation within the model's native computation, reframing memory from a passive record of prior context into an active substrate for maintaining and evolving model behavior. Evaluations across general capabilities, long-context understanding, retrieval, and efficiency further validate Safin-1. These findings provide a path toward safety as a state-native and adaptively maintainable capability. This work is only an initial architectural exploration of Safety from Within, and substantial further work is needed to realize this broader vision.

</details>


### [228] [Local Reference Geometry Residual Augmentation for Imbalanced Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00093)
*Chuanhang Qiu,Yanran Xu,Yue Wang,Anthony Bagnall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部参考几何（LRG），一种轻量级、后置的特征增强模块，用于修复不平衡时间序列分类中表示层面的"训练局部几何失效"问题——即少数类样本在特征空间中落入稀疏、被多数类主导或混合类别的邻域，即使全局类结构仍然可用。LRG仅用训练特征，测量局部暴露度与类混合风险，然后通过标准化符号位移和LDA投影残差增强每个固定特征。实验显示在UCR基准上对多种表示和组合干预均有增益，消融支持局部几何失效假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不平衡时间序列分类方法（重采样、改目标函数、改logits、调阈值）均未在表示层面回答一个关键问题：当少数类支持度降低时，学习到的特征空间在少数类周围是否保持局部可靠？作者识别出在失衡条件下，即使表示保留有用全局类结构，少数类样本仍可能落入稀疏、多数类主导或混合的特征邻域，这种"训练局部几何失效"是此前未被度量的偏差来源。

Method: 提出Local Reference Geometry（LRG），一种后置特征增强模块，插入在固定特征提取器与分类头之间。LRG使用训练特征仅做：(1) 度量每个样本的局部暴露度与类混合风险；(2) 在原始特征上附加标准化符号位移（反映附近训练几何）以及LDA投影的残差摘要。该模块为后置式（post-hoc），可与训练级干预和分类器修正组合使用。

Result: 在受控UCR/Bake Off Redux不平衡基准上，逐对raw-versus-LRG对比显示对学习型、预训练型和固定表示均有增益，且可与训练级干预及编码器后分类器修正叠加。消融实验表明增益源自有符号的局部残差附加到原始特征，而非通用原型距离、亲和特征、标量统计或VLAD风格编码。进一步分析验证了局部几何失效假说：少数类邻域在失衡下越来越被多数类暴露，训练局部风险可识别易错区域，LRG增益集中在这些高风险区域。

Conclusion: 不平衡时间序列分类不仅需要修正训练分布、目标、logits或阈值，还需要修复特征空间在少数类邻域的局部可靠性。LRG作为一种轻量级后置模块能有效诊断并修复此失效，且其作用机制与假说一致——修复局部邻域结构而非依赖通用特征变换。

Abstract: Imbalanced time series classification is often addressed by changing the training distribution, objective, logits, or final threshold. These interventions address important biases, yet leave a representation-level question unmeasured: after minority support is reduced, does a learned feature space remain locally reliable around minority regions? We identify a training-local geometry failure: under imbalance, minority cases can lie in sparse, rest-dominated, or mixed feature-space neighborhoods, even when the representation retains useful global class structure. To diagnose and repair this failure, we propose Local Reference Geometry (LRG), a lightweight post-hoc feature augmentation module applied between a fixed feature extractor and the classifier head. Using training features only, LRG measures local exposure and class-mixture risk, then augments each fixed feature with a standardized signed displacement from nearby training geometry and an LDA-projected residual summary. On controlled UCR/Bake Off Redux imbalance benchmarks, paired raw-versus-LRG comparisons show gains for learned, pretrained, and fixed representations, including when LRG is combined with training-level interventions and post-encoder classifier corrections. Ablations show that the gain comes from the signed local residual appended to the original feature, rather than from generic prototype distances, affinity features, scalar statistics, or VLAD-style codes. Further analyses support the proposed local-geometry failure hypothesis: minority neighborhoods become increasingly rest-exposed under imbalance, training-local risk identifies error-prone regions, and LRG gains concentrate in those high-risk regions.

</details>


### [229] [Faster Than Flash: Exploiting Attention Sparsity for Efficient Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00097)
*Zhigeng Liu,Zhiyuan Ning,Ruixiao Li,Xiaoran Liu,Yuerong Song,Min Zhang,Ziwei He,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FFD（Faster Flash Decoding）是一个硬件-算法协同设计的框架，通过将选择器和计算器融合为全融合内核、利用低比特量化的内容感知扫描替代外部元数据索引，并引入top-delta动态稀疏策略，突破长上下文解码中的内存墙瓶颈，实现最高11.6倍内核级加速和2.37倍端到端吞吐提升，支持256K上下文长度且保持模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文大语言模型的解码过程受限于内存带宽瓶颈和注意力机制的二次复杂度。现有的基于元数据的指标框架存在内存开销问题，而自适应选择策略则在计算效率上有所欠缺，两者之间存在固有的权衡。作者旨在提出一种方案来克服这一权衡并突破长上下文解码中的内存墙限制。

Method: FFD采用硬件-算法协同设计框架，核心包括：(1) 将选择器(selector)和计算器(computer)集成为全融合内核，通过低比特量化实现内容感知扫描以替代外部元数据索引；(2) 提出top-delta策略，动态过滤块以实现分布自适应稀疏性，且无需全局同步；(3) 实现扫描结果的计算复用，是一个免训练、即插即用的解决方案。

Result: FFD在实验中实现了最高11.6倍的内核级加速，可扩展到256K上下文长度，并带来2.37倍的端到端吞吐率提升。在RULER和LongBench基准上的实证验证表明，FFD在保持模型精度的同时实现了高比例稀疏性。

Conclusion: FFD通过硬件-算法协同设计的全融合内核与top-delta自适应稀疏策略，有效解决了长上下文解码中元数据开销与选择效率的权衡问题，在无需训练和即插即用的前提下显著提升了解码速度和上下文扩展能力，同时不牺牲模型精度。

Abstract: The development of long-context Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory bandwidth bottleneck and quadratic complexity of the attention mechanism during decoding. To overcome the inherent trade-offs between the memory overhead of metadata-based metrics and the computational inefficiency of adaptive selection strategies, we present Faster Flash Decoding (FFD), a novel hardware-algorithm co-design framework designed to break the memory wall in long-context decoding. FFD integrates the selector and computer into a fully fused kernel, replacing external metadata indices with content-aware scanning via low-bit quantization. Furthermore, we introduce the top-delta strategy, which dynamically filters blocks to achieve distribution-adaptive sparsity without global synchronization. Offering a training-free and plug-and-play solution, FFD also enables the reuse of scanning results for computation, achieving up to 11.6x kernel-level speedup and scaling to 256K context length, with 2.37x end-to-end throughput improvement. Empirical validation on RULER and LongBench confirms that FFD maintains model accuracy while delivering high-ratio sparsity, with code available at https://github.com/qluoluo/faster-flash-decoding

</details>


### [230] [Generative artificial intelligence for reliable mechanistic reasoning for corrosion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00099)
*Bharath M N,R K Singh Raman,Alankar Alankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向腐蚀知识合成的领域自适应检索增强生成（RAG）框架，在镁合金腐蚀数据上演示。三个开源语言模型在3,309个专家验证问答对上微调，结合混合稠密-词法检索管道，Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度0.964，上下文召回0.988。此外引入Reason Map命题图框架，通过构建生成答案与检索文献的有向证据图，检测平坦事实性指标无法暴露的因果方向反转和推断跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腐蚀约占全球GDP的4%，可靠预测对及时缓解至关重要。机器学习虽能有效预测腐蚀速率，但无法解释底层机理。在安全关键材料工程中，可靠方法不仅需要准确检索，还需要机理上可辩护的推理，而现有事实性指标无法评估这一能力。

Method: 采用领域自适应的检索增强生成框架：(1) 微调三个开源语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B、Mistral-7B），训练数据为来自840篇同行评审论文的3,309个专家验证问答对；(2) 构建混合稠密-词法检索管道；(3) 提出Reason Map命题图框架，从生成答案和检索文献中独立构建有向证据图，系统性检测因果方向反转和不受支持的推断跳跃。

Result: 检索增强带来Token F1提升143-194%，系统忠实度0.964，上下文召回0.988。盲法外部验证（基于新发表文献和内部电化学数据）确认了趋势级泛化能力。Reason Map能有效检测平坦事实性指标无法暴露的因果方向反转和推断跳跃。

Conclusion: 该模块化架构具有跨领域泛化能力，为可信赖的AI辅助知识合成提供了通用蓝图，不仅适用于腐蚀领域，还可扩展至其他工程领域。

Abstract: Corrosion accounts for approximately 4% of global GDP, and reliable prediction is essential for timely mitigation. Machine learning effectively predicts corrosion rates from composition, microstructure, and environmental variables, but cannot explain the underlying mechanisms. A reliable approach in safety-critical materials engineering requires not only accurate retrieval but also mechanistically defensible reasoning, a capability that existing factuality metrics cannot assess. This work presents a domain-adapted retrieval-augmented generation framework for corrosion knowledge synthesis, demonstrated on magnesium alloy corrosion. Three open-weight language models (Llama-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, Mistral-7B) are fine-tuned on 3,309 expert-verified question-answer pairs from 840 peer-reviewed papers and integrated with a hybrid dense-lexical retrieval pipeline. Retrieval augmentation produces Token F1 gains of 143-194%, with system faithfulness of 0.964 and context recall of 0.988. Blind external validation on newly published literature and in-house electrochemical data confirms trend-level generalisation. Reason Map, a proposition-graph framework, is further introduced; it independently constructs directed evidence graphs from generated answers and retrieved literature, enabling systematic detection of causal direction inversions and unsupported inferential leaps that flat factuality metrics cannot expose. The modular architecture can be applied across domains, offering a generalizable blueprint for trustworthy AI-assisted knowledge synthesis to circumvent corrosion, which can also be applied to other engineering domains.

</details>


### [231] [Good Memory Has ECC: Evaluating the Memory of Vision-Language Models Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00103)
*Shmuel Berman,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECCBbench基准与评估协议，通过效率（FLOPs）、压缩（可压缩输入是否更优）和校准（基于不确定性的弃权）三个维度衡量LLM/VLM的记忆能力，而非仅看原始准确率。研究发现预训练VLM在文本上能压缩记忆但视频上不能，且两者校准均较差；多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM/VLM记忆基准仅通过长文本或长视频上的准确率来评估，但准确率单一指标忽略了长时程任务中真正重要的记忆属性，如计算效率、可压缩性和不确定性校准。作者认为需要超越系统容量（特定预算下的原始准确率）来衡量记忆能力。

Method: 引入ECCBench基准和评估协议，定义ECC三个评估轴：Efficiency（回答记忆所需FLOPs）、Compression（可压缩输入是否被更准确或高效地记忆）、Calibration（系统是否根据自身不确定性和错误代价进行弃权）。作者对预训练VLM以及更广泛的记忆骨干架构进行系统评估。

Result: 预训练VLM在文本记忆上表现出压缩能力，但视频记忆上缺乏压缩，且在两类输入上校准均表现不佳。在更广泛的记忆骨干中，多种非Transformer架构在压缩-校准权衡上优于RoPE Transformer，暗示它们可能更适合长时程智能体应用。

Conclusion: 记忆评估不应仅依赖准确率，需要从效率、压缩和校准三个维度综合衡量。非Transformer记忆架构在关键权衡上表现更优，可能成为长时程智能体的有效组件，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Memory is widely viewed as an important unsolved problem for LLMs and VLMs, and current benchmarks typically evaluate it by testing accuracy over long text or video. However, accuracy alone misses properties that matter for real long-horizon tasks. We introduce ECCBench, a benchmark and evaluation protocol that measures memory beyond a system's capacity--its raw accuracy at a specific budget--via three axes we call ECC: efficiency--the computation, in FLOPs, needed to answer from memory; compression--whether compressible inputs are remembered more accurately or efficiently; and calibration--whether the system abstains in response to its own uncertainty and the cost of an error. We find that pretrained VLMs compress their memory over text but not video and are poorly calibrated on both. Among a broader set of memory backbones, several non-Transformer architectures achieve better compression-calibration tradeoffs than RoPE Transformers, suggesting they may be useful components for agents operating over long horizons.

</details>


### [232] [Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00129)
*J. M. Diederik Kruijssen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"缺陷自然、进化完美"（Flawed-in-Nature）机制：通过对AI/ML模型系数施加故意的突变（使其偏离最优），形成模型群组作为对抗环境非平稳性的统计对冲，从而在环境漂移时可靠且可持续地提升整体性能。通过四个定理证明其遗憾（regret）减少在一般条件下有保证，并在合成线性回归任务上验证，突变群组在约80%的环境变化中能提供最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界问题常发生不可预测的漂移（非平稳性），导致AI/ML模型性能下降。生物进化通过自然选择作用于遗传变异克服了这一障碍；尽管AI/ML早已引入自然选择思想，但优化过程中的自然收敛使得维持模型多样性极为困难，需要一种能同时兼顾多样性与性能的机制来应对环境变化。

Method: 提出Flawed-in-Nature机制：对模型群组（swarm）中的个体系数施以刻意偏离最优的突变，使个体性能受损但群组整体充当对抗非平稳性的统计对冲；利用四个定理在一般条件下证明该机制能保证遗憾（regret）减少；设计自适应控制器，通过调节突变漂移率以匹配未知的环境漂移率；在合成线性回归任务上验证模型群组性能及推理合成（inference synthesis）将个体优势转化为集体优势的效果。

Result: 实验表明，突变群组在约80%的环境变化中能提供最佳模型，并且推理合成能成功地将个体最佳优势转化为集体性能增益。机制在突变漂移率与环境漂移率匹配时效果最佳。四个定理确立了一般的遗憾减少保证。

Conclusion: Flawed-in-Nature机制可作为AI/ML系统的一般化设计原则，其与生物进化的紧密类比暗示该机制可能是有机发现更接近生物智能的AI形式所缺失的关键成分。文章提供了理论基础与实用自适应控制器，为在非平稳环境中稳健运行的AI系统设计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The performance of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) models degrades when the problem they were trained on drifts. This is a near-universal feature of real-world problems, which often change unpredictably. Biological evolution has achieved intelligence by overcoming this obstacle through natural selection acting on heritable variation. AI/ML techniques have long incorporated forms of natural selection, but it has been challenging to maintain model diversity as optimization naturally drives convergence. Here we show that a swarm of AI/ML models subjected to deliberate mutations of their model coefficients away from optimality can reliably and sustainably improve performance in changing environments by acting as a statistical hedge against non-stationarity. We call this mechanism 'Flawed in Nature, Perfect through Evolution', reflecting that the collective performance gain goes at the expense of individual performance. We prove via four theorems that the resulting regret reduction is guaranteed under general conditions, establishing the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism as a generalizable design principle for AI/ML systems. We validate these results on synthetic linear regression tasks, demonstrating that the mutated swarm delivers the best model in $\sim80\%$ of environment changes and that inference synthesis successfully translates this individual advantage into a collective one. The mechanism proves to be most effective when the mutation drift rate matches the drift rate of the environment. We outline a simple, adaptive controller that enables practical applications by tuning the mutation drift rate to match the unknown drift rate of the environment. The close analogy of the Flawed-in-Nature mechanism to biological evolution suggests it may have been a critical missing ingredient for the organic discovery of AI forms that more closely mimic biological intelligence.

</details>


### [233] [Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning for Goal-Directed Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00189)
*Shiyun Wa,Yifei Wang,Anna G. Green,Simone Sciabola,Ye Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT)，一种不依赖轨迹级强化学习的目标导向分子生成优化方法。该方法通过奖励指导的精英选择筛选高分分子，并仅使用模型原生预训练损失进行更新，统一适用于自回归、掩码扩散和离散流等多种生成架构，以及de novo、motif扩展和linker设计等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导向分子优化通常依赖策略梯度强化学习，需要计算生成轨迹的对数概率，其形式依赖模型架构和生成过程，导致优化器难以在不同架构和条件生成设计之间复用。监督微调虽然无需这些机制，但其更新由固定数据集驱动，奖励信号无法进入更新过程。因此需要一个统一且有效的优化框架。

Method: EW-SFT使用奖励来指导精英选择（elite selection），筛选出高评分分子作为训练集，然后仅通过模型自身的预训练损失在该选定集合上进行更新。消融实验表明奖励信息主要通过精英选择传递，而非在选定集合内进行连续加权。该方法的更新仅消耗评分分子和模型原生损失，因此与具体架构无关。

Result: 在固定预算的3D形状对齐oracle调用次数下，EW-SFT在两种激酶参考化合物上持续优于对应的原生优化器。在4个留出参考化合物的2D相似性oracle下进一步改善目标导向优化，并在无需轨迹级RL公式的样本效率基准上取得可比性能。EW-SFT在多种分子生成器、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle上均表现出一致的优越性。

Conclusion: EW-SFT是一种统一且有效的分子生成优化器，跨越不同的生成架构、设计约束、参考化合物和oracle均能取得优异表现，展示了将奖励信号注入监督微调过程的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Goal-directed optimization is essential for steering molecular generators to propose candidates with desired properties. However, it is often implemented with policy-gradient reinforcement learning, which requires a generation-trajectory log-probability whose form depends on the model architecture and generation procedure. This makes an optimizer difficult to reuse across architectures and conditional generative designs. Supervised fine-tuning needs none of that machinery, but its update is driven by a fixed dataset, so the reward never enters the update. We introduce Elite-Weighted Supervised Fine-tuning (EW-SFT), which uses reward to guide elite selection of high-scoring molecules, and updates the model by its own pretraining loss on that set. Ablations show that reward information is passed primarily through elite selection, rather than through continuous weighting within the selected set. Because the update consumes only scored molecules and the model's native loss, the same rule applies across autoregressive, masked-diffusion, and discrete-flow generators, and across de novo, motif-extension, and linker-design tasks. Under a fixed budget of 3D shape alignment oracle calls on two kinase reference compounds, EW-SFT consistently outperforms the corresponding native optimizers. It further improves goal-directed optimization under a 2D similarity oracle on four held-out references and achieves comparable performance on a sample-efficiency benchmark without a trajectory-level RL formulation. These results demonstrate that EW-SFT is a unified and effective optimizer across molecular generators, design constraints, references, and oracles.

</details>


### [234] [WHALE: A Simple Recipe for Joint Harness-Weight Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00196)
*Haechan Kim,Yoonho Lee,Gisang Lee,Chelsea Finn,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WHALE（Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning）交替学习框架，在模型参数与可执行harness代码之间进行联合优化，交替执行模型微调与harness搜索两个阶段，在Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体上较基线方法在三个域中提升4.15-24.38个百分点的best mean@8准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的性能由模型参数和可执行harness代码（管理上下文与流程控制）共同决定。单独优化任一组件会被另一个冻结的组件拖累成为瓶颈：权重更新可能改变有效harness，harness更新也会影响暴露的模型能力。现有联合自适应方法只优化权重和文本提示词，而将更广泛的harness固定不变，缺乏真正的联合优化方案。

Method: WHALE采用两个交替阶段：在当前harness下用在线拒绝采样微调更新模型，然后在更新后的模型下用Meta-Harness搜索更优harness。切换时机是关键设计选择：采用固定阶段时长或基于训练信号的自适应耐心规则，以区分真实改进与噪声，同时避免对动态变化的对端过度优化。另对比了交错小步更新与stagewise（先权重后harness）优化的差异。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-2B/4B智能体上，跨越三个领域（搜索问答、数学推理、国际象棋谜题），WHALE较仅优化权重、仅优化harness以及Fast-Slow Training方法在best mean@8准确率上提升4.15-24.38个百分点。实验还显示任一组分都可能是瓶颈：harness搜索在SearchQA中以远少rollout达到峰值仅权重优化的精度，但数学准确率需要先权重更新才能提升；交错的小步更新在准确率与rollout成本上均优于stagewise的先权重后harness优化。

Conclusion: WHALE验证了模型权重与harness代码的联合交替优化优于任何单一维度优化或顺序式优化，且阶段切换策略（固定或自适应规则）对去噪与平衡双方优化至关重要。

Abstract: Agent performance depends jointly on the model parameters and the executable harness code that manages context and control flow. Optimizing either component in isolation can leave the system bottlenecked by its frozen counterpart: weight updates can change which harness is effective, while harness updates can change which model capabilities are exposed. Existing joint-adaptation methods optimize weights and textual prompts but leave the broader harness fixed. We propose Weight-Harness Alternating LEarning (WHALE), a simple recipe that alternates two phases: updating the model under the current harness, then searching for a better harness under the updated model. We instantiate these two phases with online rejection-sampling fine-tuning and Meta-Harness, respectively. When to switch is a key design choice: to separate real improvements from noise without over-optimizing against a changing counterpart, WHALE uses either fixed phase durations or an adaptive patience rule over training signals. Using Qwen3.5-2B/4B agents across three domains (search question answering, mathematical reasoning, and chess puzzles), WHALE outperforms weight-only, harness-only, and Fast-Slow Training by 4.15-24.38 percentage points in best mean@8 accuracy. Either component can be the bottleneck: harness search matches peak weight-only accuracy with far fewer rollouts in SearchQA, but improves math accuracy only after a weight update. Small interleaved updates also outperform stagewise weight-then-harness optimization in accuracy and rollout cost. The code is available at https://github.com/krafton-ai/WHALE.

</details>


### [235] [QTEA: Ternary LLMs with Sparse Residual Salient Weight and By-Column Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00224)
*Yipin Guo,Arun M George,Jie Fu,Tareq Mahmoud,Sixue Xing,Siddharth Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QTEA是一个面向大语言模型的亚2比特仅权重量化（PTQ）框架，将权重量化为三值，使用半结构化1:4稀疏的显著列分配残差作为误差补偿器，并引入列级rescaling重标定与错误衰减解决GPTQ排序依赖的误差传播问题。在Qwen3-14B上达到有效1.7比特/权重，相较最强三值PTQ基线平均准确率提升16.7%；在Llama3-8B上获得6.6%准确率提升，并开发了基于查找表的推理核，比FP16基线快7.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM权重PTQ方法在低于2比特量化下严重精度损失且难以跨模型泛化；虽有无结构稀疏缓解损失，但牺牲了规则性与GPU友好执行效率。目标是在亚2比特场景下兼顾低比特精度与硬件效率。

Method: QTEA包含三个核心组件：(1) 将权重量化为三值{+1,0,-1}，并用显著权重作为残差误差补偿器；为保持硬件效率，残差仅在显著列内以半结构化1:4稀疏模式分配。(2) 对GPTQ式逐列量化引入列级rescaling细化，交替更新每列缩放因子与三值分配以减少重建误差。(3) 识别GPTQ中顺序依赖的误差传播问题，提出错误衰减机制抑制后期误差累积。此外开发了基于查找表的推理内核加速生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-14B上达到有效1.7比特/权重，相对最强三值PTQ基线平均准确率提升16.7%，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低1.40倍和2.61倍；在Llama3-8B上准确率提升6.6%，两数据集困惑度分别降低1.34倍和1.95倍；查找表内核实现较FP16基线7.2倍的逐token生成加速。

Conclusion: QTEA通过三值量化+半结构化残差补偿+列级缩放优化+误差衰减，在亚2比特PTQ下同时实现了高精度泛化能力与GPU友好的硬件效率，验证了在三值/亚2比特极低比特量化场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Weight-only post-training quantization (PTQ) can alleviate the computational burden of serving large language models (LLMs) at scale. However, existing PTQ methods often fail to generalize across models and suffer severe accuracy loss below 2 bits. Many leverage unstructured sparsity to mitigate this loss, but at the cost of regularity and GPU-friendly execution. We present QTEA, a sub-2-bit PTQ framework that quantizes weights into ternary values and uses salient weights as residual error compensators. To maintain hardware efficiency, residuals are assigned to selected columns with semi-structured \(1{:}4\) sparsity within the salient columns. We further add column-wise rescale refinement to GPTQ-style column-by-column quantization, alternately updating per-column scales and ternary assignments to reduce reconstruction error. We also identify order-dependent error propagation in GPTQ and introduce error decay to attenuate late-stage error accumulation. On Qwen3-14B, QTEA compresses all weights to an effective 1.7 bits per weight while improving average accuracy over the strongest ternary PTQ baseline by 16.7\%. It also achieves 1.40\(\times\) and 2.61\(\times\) lower perplexity on WikiText and C4 respectively. This trend holds on Llama3-8B, where QTEA obtains a 6.6\% accuracy gain and 1.34\(\times\)/1.95\(\times\) lower perplexity on the same datasets. Finally, we develop a lookup-table based kernel that achieves 7.2\(\times\) faster per-token generation over an FP16 baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Intelligent-Microsystems-Lab/QTEA.

</details>


### [236] [Geometry-aware Latent Autoregressive Generative Model for PDEs in Complex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00297)
*Zi Wang,Minghui Xu,Tapan Mukerji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的潜在自回归生成模型（GeoLAMP），用于在高度复杂且扭曲的微米级几何结构中求解多物理场偏微分方程。GeoLAMP采用双编码器架构联合捕获全局拓扑和细尺度几何特征，通过因果自注意力Transformer与流匹配在潜在空间中建模时间动态，实现稳定且可扩展的分块自回归预测。在反应流、热对流和弹性力学的三个多物理场基准数据集上，GeoLAMP在完整推理视界内保持了最低的误差和最优的自回归稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在能源和化学工程中，多物理场PDE的求解在高度复杂的微米级蜿蜒几何结构中是一项重大挑战，传统数值方法在如此不规则和扭曲的几何条件下计算成本高昂且难以稳定求解。

Method: GeoLAMP包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于图表示的双编码器架构，同时捕获全局拓扑结构和细尺度几何特征，实现从实空间场到紧凑潜在表示的有效转换；(2) 在潜在空间中采用带流匹配的因果自注意力Transformer，实现稳定且可扩展的分块自回归时间预测；(3) 灵活的解码器可在任意点重建高分辨率物理场。

Result: 作者建立了三个微米级复杂几何中的多物理场基准数据集（反应流、热对流、弹性力学）。GeoLAMP在所有数据集上均实现了最稳定的自回归性能，在完整rollout视界内始终维持低误差。

Conclusion: 该工作系统研究了复杂几何中的几何感知PDE学习，为通过流匹配框架对潜在自回归PDE建模进行分块时间推进提供了新的见解。

Abstract: Solving multiphysics partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a major challenge in scientific computing, especially for highly complex $μ$m-scale tortuous geometries critical to energy and chemical engineering.
